## 🌐 第一阶段综合任务：认识一位新同学

> **任务目标：** 用简单句完成一次自我介绍和基本问答。建议分两天完成，不查答案直接输出。

### 1. 听力：抓住个人信息

请让学习伙伴朗读下文，或把英文部分交给文本转语音工具播放两遍。听的时候不要看原文，记录姓名、城市、职业、爱好和作息。

> Hi, I'm Emma. I live in Bristol and work in a small hotel. I usually start work at eight o'clock. I like music, but I don't play an instrument. On Sundays, I visit my parents or read at home.
>
> 你好，我是艾玛。我住在布里斯托尔，在一家小旅馆工作。我通常八点开始工作。我喜欢音乐，但不会演奏乐器。星期日，我会去看父母或在家读书。

听后回答：Where does Emma work?（艾玛在哪里工作？）What time does she start work?（她几点开始工作？）What does she do on Sundays?（她星期日做什么？）

### 2. 阅读：辨认句子骨架

阅读上面的介绍，为每句话圈出主语和谓语，再找出一个否定句和一个由 `or` 连接的并列结构。然后不看中文，用一句英文概括 Emma 的生活。

### 3. 口语：60 秒自我介绍

录制一段45至60秒的自我介绍，至少包括以下内容：

- 姓名和所在城市
- 学习或工作
- 两个日常习惯
- 一个喜欢和一个不喜欢的事物
- 一个向听者提出的问题

可以使用：`My name is...`、`I live...`、`I usually...`、`I like..., but I don't like...`、`What do you...?`

### 4. 写作：制作个人资料

写6至8句英文个人资料，至少使用一次 `be` 动词、一般现在时、第三人称单数、否定句和疑问句。完成后朗读一遍，检查主语变化时动词是否也发生了变化。

**达标标准：** 能听出至少4项信息；口语连续表达45秒；写作包含6个完整句子；基础检查正确率达到80%。未达标时先重做，不急着进入第二阶段。

---
---

# 第二阶段 · 基础语法

> **本阶段目标：** 能清楚表达过去、现在和将来的动作，并掌握常见的情态、比较和非谓语表达。
>
> **学习提醒：** 看到时间词先想“动作发生在什么时候”，再选择时态；不要只靠关键词机械套用。

---

## 18. 一般过去时

### 讲解

**用途：** 表示①**过去某时发生**的动作；②过去**经常性**的动作；③过去的状态。

### 18.1 结构

| 句型 | be 动词 | 实义动词 |
|---|---|---|
| 肯定 | I **was** happy. | I **worked** here. |
| 否定 | I **wasn't** happy. | I **didn't work** here. |
| 一般疑问 | **Were** you happy? | **Did** you work here? |
| 特殊疑问 | Why **were** you late? | Where **did** you work? |
| 简答 | Yes, I **was**. / No, I **wasn't**. | Yes, I **did**. / No, I **didn't**. |

**⚠️ 核心规则：用了 `did`，后面动词必须还原成原形！**
- ❌ Did you went there? → ✅ Did you **go** there? 你去那里了吗？
- ❌ He didn't went. → ✅ He didn't **go**. 他没去。

### ✍️ 练习 18.1：一般过去时结构

填空：We ____ (finish) the meeting yesterday

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`finished`

</details>

### 18.2 动词过去式的规则变化

| 情况 | 规则 | 例子 |
|---|---|---|
| 一般情况 | +ed | work → worked, play → played |
| 以 e 结尾 | +d | like → liked, live → lived |
| 辅音字母 + y 结尾 | 变 y 为 i + ed | study → studied, carry → carried |
| 元音字母 + y 结尾 | +ed | play → played, stay → stayed |
| 重读闭音节，末尾一个辅音字母 | 双写 + ed | stop → stop**p**ed, plan → plan**n**ed, prefer → prefer**r**ed |

**-ed 发音规则：**

| 词尾发音 | -ed 读音 | 例子 |
|---|---|---|
| 清辅音（除 /t/）后 | /t/ | worked /wɜːkt/, stopped /stɒpt/ |
| 浊辅音和元音（除 /d/）后 | /d/ | played /pleɪd/, lived /lɪvd/ |
| /t/ /d/ 后 | /ɪd/ | wanted /wɒntɪd/, needed /niːdɪd/ |

### ✍️ 练习 18.2：过去式规则变化

写出过去式：`plan`；`study`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`planned`；`study` → `studied`。

</details>

### 18.3 不规则动词（必背，完整表见附录 B）

**常见分类记忆：**

| 类型 | 例子 |
|---|---|
| AAA（三个一样） | put-put-put, cut-cut-cut, let-let-let, cost-cost-cost, read-read-read（读音变） |
| ABA（原形=过去分词） | come-came-come, run-ran-run, become-became-become |
| ABB（过去式=过去分词） | buy-bought-bought, teach-taught-taught, think-thought-thought, bring-brought-brought |
| ABC（三个都不同） | go-went-gone, do-did-done, see-saw-seen, write-wrote-written, eat-ate-eaten |

### ✍️ 练习 18.3：不规则过去式

填空：She ____ (go) home early

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`went`

</details>

### 18.4 标志词

`yesterday, last night/week/month/year, ... ago, in 2010, just now, at that time, once upon a time, in the past, when + 过去时从句`

**⚠️ 注意 `just now`（刚才）用**过去时**，`just`（刚刚）用**现在完成时**。

### ✍️ 练习 18.4：过去时间标志词

选择：I called him (last night / now)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`last night`

</details>

### 18.5 used to 与 would（过去习惯）

| 结构 | 含义 | 用法 |
|---|---|---|
| used to + 动词原形 | 过去常常（现在不再） | 可表**状态**或**动作** |
| would + 动词原形 | 过去常常 | 只表**动作**，不表状态 |

- I **used to** live in Beijing.（状态，只能用 used to）
- He **used to / would** go fishing on Sundays.（动作，都可以）

**否定与疑问：**
- He **didn't use to** smoke. / He **used not to** smoke. 他过去不抽烟。
- **Did** he **use to** smoke? / **Used** he **to** smoke? 他过去抽烟吗？

**⚠️ 三个 used 别混：**
1. `used to do` = 过去常常做
2. `be used to doing` = 习惯于做
3. `be used to do` = 被用来做（被动）

- He **used to smoke**. 他以前抽烟。
- He **is used to smoking**. 他习惯抽烟。
- Wood **is used to make** paper. 木头被用来造纸。

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **did 后动词没还原**：❌ Did he came? → ✅ Did he **come**?
2. **过去式和过去分词混用**：❌ I have went. → ✅ I have **gone**.
3. **双写规则**：❌ stoped → ✅ stop**p**ed
4. **be 动词和 did 混用**：❌ Did you were there? → ✅ **Were** you there?

翻译：我过去每天早起

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I used to get up early every day.`

</details>

## 19. 过去进行时

### 讲解

**用途：** 表示**过去某一时刻或某段时间正在进行**的动作。

### 19.1 结构

**核心公式：was/were + 动词-ing**

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 肯定 | I **was reading** at 8 last night. |
| 否定 | I **wasn't reading**. |
| 一般疑问 | **Were** you **reading**? |
| 特殊疑问 | What **were** you **doing**? |
| 简答 | Yes, I **was**. / No, I **wasn't**. |

### ✍️ 练习 19.1：过去进行时结构

填空：At eight, I ____ ____ (read)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`was reading`

</details>

### 19.2 标志词

`at this time yesterday, at 8 o'clock last night, this time last week, when + 过去时, while + 过去进行时`

### ✍️ 练习 19.2：过去进行时标志词

选择：At that moment, she (was speaking / spoke)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`was speaking`

</details>

### 19.3 when / while 的经典搭配（超高频考点）

| 结构 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **When** + 过去时，过去进行时 | 当……时，正在…… | **When** I **arrived**, he **was cooking**. |
| **While** + 过去进行时，过去时 | 正当……时，突然…… | **While** I **was reading**, the phone **rang**. |
| 过去进行时 **when** 过去时 | 正在……突然…… | I **was reading when** the phone **rang**. |
| 两个 while 过去进行时 | 两个动作同时进行 | **While** I **was cooking**, she **was cleaning**. |

**记忆口诀：**
- **短动作**（arrive, come, ring, happen）→ 用**一般过去时**
- **长动作**（背景动作）→ 用**过去进行时**
- `when` 后常接**短动作**，`while` 后常接**长动作**

### ✍️ 练习 19.3：when / while

完成：While I was working, he ____ (call)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`called`

</details>

### 19.4 过去进行时的其他用法

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 表过去将来（计划） | He said he **was leaving** the next day. |
| 委婉语气 | I **was wondering** if you could help me. 不知您能否帮忙。 |
| always + 过去进行时（情感） | He **was always complaining**. 他老是抱怨。 |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **状态动词不用进行时**（同现在进行时规则）
2. **when / while 后的时态选错**
3. **忘记 was/were**：❌ I reading when... → ✅ I **was** reading when...

### ✍️ 练习 19.4：过去进行时综合

翻译：昨晚九点我们正在开会

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`We were having a meeting at nine last night.`

</details>

用适当时态填空：

1. I ___ (watch) TV when he ___ (come) in. 他进来的时候我正在看电视。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：was watching / came

</details>
2. While she ___ (cook), the phone ___ (ring). 她做饭的时候电话响了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：was cooking / rang

</details>
3. What ___ you ___ (do) at 8 o'clock last night? 你昨晚8点在做什么？

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：were / doing

</details>
4. They ___ (play) football at this time yesterday. 他们昨天这个时候在踢足球。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：were playing

</details>
5. When the teacher ___ (enter), the students ___ (talk). 老师进来的时候，学生们正在说话。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：entered / were talking

</details>

改错：

6. ❌ While I was reading, the door was opening suddenly. 我正在读书的时候，门突然开了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ While I was reading, the door **opened** suddenly.

</details>
7. ❌ I reading a book when you called me. 你给我打电话的时候我正在读书。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ I **was reading** a book when you called me.

</details>

## 20. 一般将来时

### 讲解

**用途：** 表示**将来要发生**的动作或状态。有 5 种表达方式，含义有细微差别。

### 20.1 五种将来时表达法（重点对比）

| 结构 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **will / shall + 动词原形** | 单纯将来、临时决定、承诺、预测 | I **will help** you. 我会帮你。 |
| **be going to + 动词原形** | 打算、计划；有迹象的推测 | I **am going to** buy a car. 我打算买车。 |
| **现在进行时** | 按计划安排（位移动词） | I **am leaving** tomorrow. |
| **一般现在时** | 时刻表、日程表 | The train **leaves** at 6. |
| **be about to + 动词原形** | 即将（马上）；**不能与具体时间连用** | He **is about to** leave. |
| **be to + 动词原形** | 计划、命令、注定 | The meeting **is to** be held tomorrow. |

**will 的完整句型（最常用的一种）：**

| 句型 | 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 肯定 | 主语 + **will** + 动词原形 | He **will come** tomorrow. 他明天会来。 |
| 否定 | 主语 + **won't**(will not) + 原形 | He **won't come**. 他不会来。 |
| 一般疑问 | **Will** + 主语 + 原形? | **Will** he **come**? 他会来吗？ |
| 特殊疑问 | 疑问词 + **will** + 主语 + 原形? | When **will** he **come**? 他什么时候来？ |
| 简答 | Yes, 主语 + will. / No, 主语 + won't. | Yes, he **will**. / No, he **won't**. |

**be going to 的句型（靠 be 变化，不用 do）：**

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 肯定 | I **am going to** buy a car. 我打算买车。 |
| 否定 | I **am not going to** buy a car. 我不打算买车。 |
| 一般疑问 | **Are** you **going to** buy a car? 你打算买车吗？ |
| 简答 | Yes, I **am**. / No, I'm **not**. |

### ✍️ 练习 20.1：将来时表达

翻译：我明天会回复邮件

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I will reply to the email tomorrow.`

</details>

### 20.2 will 与 be going to 的区别（高频考点）

| 对比 | will | be going to |
|---|---|---|
| 决定时间 | **说话时**临时决定 | **说话前**已计划好 |
| 例句 | — The phone is ringing. — I'**ll** get it. | I'**m going to** visit my aunt this weekend. |
| 推测依据 | 主观判断、认为 | **有客观迹象** |
| 例句 | I think it **will** rain. | Look at the clouds! It'**s going to** rain. |

### ✍️ 练习 20.2：will 与 be going to

选择：Look at those clouds. It (will / is going to) rain

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`is going to`

</details>

### 20.3 will 的其他用法

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 表意愿 | I **will** always love you. |
| 表请求（疑问句） | **Will** you close the door? |
| 表习惯性（"总会"） | Oil **will** float on water. 油总是浮在水上。 |
| 表拒绝（否定） | The door **won't** open. 门打不开。 |

### ✍️ 练习 20.3：will 的其他用法

翻译：请把文件发给我

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Will you send me the file?`

</details>

### 20.4 shall 的用法

- **征求意见**（用于 I / we）：**Shall** I open the window? 我把窗户打开好吗？
- **Shall we** go now? 我们现在走好吗？
- 正式文书表义务：You **shall** obey the rules.

### ✍️ 练习 20.4：shall

提议：我们开始会议好吗？

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Shall we start the meeting?`

</details>

### 20.5 主将从现（超高频考点）

**在时间状语从句和条件状语从句中，用一般现在时表示将来。**

**时间连词：** when, while, before, after, until, as soon as, once, by the time
**条件连词：** if, unless, as long as, in case, provided that

- ✅ I **will call** you when he **comes** back. 他回来的时候我会给你打电话。
- ❌ I will call you when he will come back. 他回来的时候我会给你打电话。
- ✅ **If** it **rains** tomorrow, we **won't** go. 如果明天下雨，我们就不去了。
- ❌ If it will rain tomorrow... 如果明天下雨……

**⚠️ 但如果 when/if 引导的是宾语从句，则可以用将来时：**
- I don't know **when** he **will come**.（when 引导宾语从句，"什么时候"）
- I'll go **when** he **comes**.（when 引导时间状语从句，"当……时"）
- I don't know **if** he **will come**.（if = 是否，宾语从句）
- **If** he **comes**, I'll tell him.（if = 如果，条件状语从句）

**判断技巧：** if 翻译成"**是否**"→ 宾语从句 → 可用将来时；if 翻译成"**如果**"→ 条件从句 → 用现在时。

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **主将从现忘记**（最高频）
2. **be going to 后接动词原形**：❌ I'm going to buying → ✅ I'm going to **buy**
3. **will 后接原形**：❌ He will goes → ✅ He will **go**
4. **be about to 不能接具体时间**：❌ He is about to leave tomorrow.

### ✍️ 练习 20.5：主将从现

填空：When he ____ (arrive), we will begin

</details>

## 21. 过去将来时

### 讲解

**用途：** 表示**从过去某时看将来**要发生的动作。常用于**宾语从句**中（主句是过去时）。

### 21.1 结构

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| would + 动词原形 | He said he **would come**. 他说他会来。 |
| was/were going to + 动词原形 | I **was going to** call you. 我本打算给你打电话。 |
| was/were about to + 动词原形 | He **was about to** leave when I arrived. |
| 过去进行时表过去将来 | He said he **was leaving** the next day. |

**would 的否定与疑问：**

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 肯定 | He said he **would come**. 他说他会来。 |
| 否定 | He said he **wouldn't come**. 他说他不会来。 |
| 疑问（在宾语从句中用陈述语序） | I asked whether he **would come**. 我问他是否会来。 |

**⚠️ 过去将来时几乎只出现在「主句是过去时」的从句里**，很少单独成句。

### ✍️ 练习 21.1：过去将来时结构

填空：He said he ____ (come) later

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`would come`

</details>

### 21.2 常见搭配

**主句过去时 → 从句用过去将来时：**

- He **said** that he **would** help me. 他说他会帮我。
- I **thought** you **would** come. 我以为你会来。
- She **told** me she **was going to** study abroad. 她告诉我她打算出国留学。

### ✍️ 练习 21.2：过去将来时搭配

翻译：她答应会帮忙

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`She promised that she would help.`

</details>

### 21.3 时间状语的变化（间接引语中）

| 直接引语 | 间接引语 |
|---|---|
| today | that day |
| tomorrow | the next day / the following day |
| yesterday | the day before / the previous day |
| next week | the next week / the following week |
| last week | the week before |
| now | then |
| here | there |
| this | that |
| ago | before |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **主句过去时，从句忘记用过去将来时**：
   - ❌ He said he will come. → ✅ He said he **would** come. 他说他会来。
2. **was going to 表示"本打算（但没做）"**：
   - I **was going to** call you, but I forgot. 我本想打给你，但忘了。

### ✍️ 练习 21.3：时间状语变化

直接引语中的 today 转述为过去时通常变为 `that day`。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：that day

</details>

1. She said she ____ (visit) us the following week

<details>

</details>
2. I knew that the meeting ____ (start) at nine

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`would start`

</details>
3. He promised he ____ (not / be) late

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`would not be`

</details>
4. They were going to leave, but the car ____ (not / start)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`would not start`

</details>

</details>

## 22. 情态动词

### 讲解

情态动词表示说话人的**语气和态度**（能力、可能、允许、义务等）。

### 22.1 情态动词的共同特点

1. **本身有意义**，但**不能单独作谓语**，必须接**动词原形**
2. **没有人称和数的变化**（不加 s）
3. **否定**直接加 not，**疑问**直接提前
4. 后面**永远跟动词原形**

- ❌ He cans swim. → ✅ He **can** swim. 他会游泳。
- ❌ He can swims. → ✅ He can **swim**. 他会游泳。

### ✍️ 练习 22.1：情态动词规则

改错：She can speaks English

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`She can speak English.`

</details>

### 22.2 主要情态动词详解

#### can / could

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 能力（能、会） | I **can** swim. |
| 许可（可以） | You **can** go now. |
| 可能性（理论上可能） | Anyone **can** make mistakes. |
| 请求（could 更礼貌） | **Could** you help me? |
| 惊讶/怀疑（疑问/否定句） | **Can** it be true? 这可能是真的吗？ |
| can't = 不可能（否定推测） | It **can't** be him. 不可能是他。 |

**can = be able to**，但 `be able to` 有更多时态：
- I **was able to** finish it.（强调"设法做到了"）
- I **will be able to** speak English well. 我将能够说一口流利的英语。

#### may / might

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 许可（正式） | **May** I come in? |
| 可能性（不确定） | He **may** come tomorrow. |
| might 语气更弱 | He **might** be at home. |
| 祝愿（书面） | **May** you succeed! 祝你成功！ |

**回答 May I...? 时：**
- 肯定：Yes, you may. / Certainly. / Sure.
- 否定：**No, you mustn't.** / No, you can't.（不用 may not）

#### must / have to

| 对比 | must | have to |
|---|---|---|
| 含义 | **主观**必须（说话人认为） | **客观**不得不（外界要求） |
| 例句 | I **must** study hard.（我自己想） | I **have to** work overtime.（老板要求） |
| 时态 | 只有现在式 | 有各种时态：had to, will have to |
| 否定 | **mustn't** = 禁止 | **don't have to** = 不必 |

**⚠️ 超高频考点：**
- `mustn't` = **禁止**（You mustn't smoke here. 这里禁止吸烟）
- `don't have to` = `needn't` = **不必**（You don't have to come. 你不必来）

**must 表推测（肯定推测，把握大）：**
- He **must be** tired.（他一定累了）
- 否定推测用 **can't**：He **can't be** tired.（他不可能累）

#### should / ought to

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 应该（建议、义务） | You **should** study harder. |
| 推测（理应） | He **should** be home by now. 他现在应该到家了。 |
| ought to 语气略强，更客观 | You **ought to** obey the law. |

#### need / dare

**既是情态动词，又是实义动词：**

| 类型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 情态动词（多用于否定/疑问） | You **needn't** go. / **Need** I go? |
| 实义动词 | You **don't need to** go. / **Do** you **need to** go? |
| 情态 dare | How **dare** you say that! |
| 实义 dare | He **doesn't dare to** speak. |

**⚠️ 经典问答：**
- **Need** I finish it today? — Yes, you **must**. / No, you **needn't**. 我今天需要完成这个吗？——是的，你必须完成。/不，你不需要。

#### shall / will / would

| 情态动词 | 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| shall | 征求意见（I/we） | **Shall** we go? |
| will | 意愿、请求 | **Will** you help me? |
| would | 委婉请求（比 will 礼貌） | **Would** you like some tea? |
| would | 过去习惯 | He **would** sit here for hours. |
| would rather | 宁愿 | I **would rather** stay home. |
| had better | 最好 | You **had better** go now. |

**⚠️ 注意固定搭配的否定形式：**
- had better **not** do
- would rather **not** do
- ❌ hadn't better / wouldn't rather

### ✍️ 练习 22.2：情态动词含义

选择：You (must / can) wear a badge here

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`must`

</details>

### 22.3 情态动词表推测（超高频考点）

**把握程度排序：must > may > might > could > can't**

| 情态动词 | 可能性 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| must be | 一定是（95%） | He **must be** at home. |
| may be | 可能是（50%） | He **may be** at home. |
| might / could be | 也许是（30%） | He **might be** at home. |
| can't be | 不可能是（否定推测） | He **can't be** at home. |

**⚠️ 疑问句中的推测用 can，不用 must：**
- **Can** he be at home? 他会在家吗？

### ✍️ 练习 22.3：情态动词表推测

选择：The light is on. She (must / can't) be in the office

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`must`

</details>

### 22.4 情态动词 + have done（对过去的推测/评价）超高频

| 结构 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **must have done** | 一定做过（肯定推测） | He **must have gone** home. 他一定回家了。 |
| **can't have done** | 不可能做过（否定推测） | He **can't have done** it. 他不可能做过。 |
| **may/might have done** | 可能做过 | He **may have forgotten**. 他可能忘了。 |
| **could have done** | ①可能做过 ②本能做但没做 | You **could have helped** me! 你本可以帮我的！ |
| **should have done** | 本应该做但没做（**责备**） | You **should have told** me. 你本该告诉我的。 |
| **shouldn't have done** | 本不该做却做了 | You **shouldn't have said** that. |
| **needn't have done** | 本不必做却做了 | You **needn't have come**. 你本不必来的。 |
| **ought to have done** | 本应该做但没做 | He **ought to have finished** it. |
| **would have done** | 本来会做（虚拟） | I **would have gone**, but I was busy. |

**⚠️ 对比记忆：**
- `needn't have done` = 做了但没必要
- `didn't need to do` = 没必要做，也确实没做

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **情态动词后加 s 或 to**：
   - ❌ He cans / He can to swim → ✅ He **can swim**
2. **mustn't 和 needn't 混用**：
   - `mustn't` = 禁止；`needn't` = 不必
3. **must 提问的否定回答**：
   - Must I go? — No, you **needn't**.（不是 mustn't）
4. **否定推测用 can't，不用 mustn't**：
   - ❌ He mustn't be at home. → ✅ He **can't be** at home. 他不可能在家。
5. **两个情态动词不能连用**：
   - ❌ He will can do it. → ✅ He **will be able to** do it. 他将能够做到这件事。
   - ❌ You must can finish it. → ✅ You must **be able to** finish it. 你必须能够完成这件事。

### ✍️ 练习 22.4：情态动词 + have done

翻译：他一定已经到家了

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He must have arrived home.`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 22

选择填空（can / may / must / should / needn't / mustn't / can't）：

1. You ___ smoke here. It's a hospital.（禁止）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：mustn't

</details>
2. You ___ hurry. There's plenty of time.（不必）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：needn't

</details>
3. He ___ be at home; his car is outside.（一定）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：must

</details>
4. He ___ be at home; I saw him at school just now.（不可能）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：can't

</details>
5. ___ I use your phone? — Yes, of course. 我可以用一下你的手机吗？——当然可以。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：May / Can

</details>
6. You ___ study harder if you want to pass. 如果你想通过考试，你必须更加努力学习。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：should / must

</details>

用情态动词 + have done 填空：

7. You ___ (tell) me earlier! I would have helped.（本该告诉我）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：should have told

</details>
8. He ___ (go) home; his coat is gone.（一定回家了）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：must have gone

</details>
9. You ___ (buy) so much food. We have enough.（本不必买）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：needn't have bought

</details>
10. He ___ (do) it; he was in Beijing at that time.（不可能做过）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：can't have done

</details>

改错：

11. ❌ He can swims very well. 他游泳游得很好。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ He can **swim** very well.

</details>
12. ❌ Must I go now? — No, you mustn't. 我现在必须走吗？——不，你不能走。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ Must I go now? — No, you **needn't**.

</details>
13. ❌ He will can help you tomorrow. 他明天将能够帮助你。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ He **will be able to** help you tomorrow.

</details>

## 23. 比较级与最高级

### 讲解

用于比较两个或多个事物的性质、程度。

### 23.1 变化规则

| 情况 | 规则 | 原级 → 比较级 → 最高级 |
|---|---|---|
| 单音节一般情况 | +er / +est | tall → taller → tallest |
| 以 e 结尾 | +r / +st | nice → nicer → nicest |
| 辅音字母 + y | 变 y 为 i + er/est | happy → happier → happiest |
| 重读闭音节，末尾一个辅音字母 | 双写 + er/est | big → big**g**er → big**g**est, hot → hotter → hottest, thin → thinner |
| 多音节/部分双音节 | more / most + 原级 | beautiful → more beautiful → most beautiful |

**不规则变化（必背）：**

| 原级 | 比较级 | 最高级 | 备注 |
|---|---|---|---|
| good / well | better | best | |
| bad / badly / ill | worse | worst | |
| many / much | more | most | |
| little | less | least | |
| far | farther / further | farthest / furthest | farther 指距离，further 指程度 |
| old | older / elder | oldest / eldest | elder 指长幼（只作定语） |
| late | later / latter | latest / last | later 指时间，latter 指顺序 |

### ✍️ 练习 23.1：比较级变化

写出比较级：`small`；`useful`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`smaller`；useful → `more useful`。

</details>

### 23.2 比较级句型（重点）

| 句型 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| A + be + 比较级 + **than** + B | A 比 B 更…… | He is **taller than** me. |
| A + be + **as** + 原级 + **as** + B | A 和 B 一样…… | He is **as tall as** me. |
| A + be + **not as/so** + 原级 + **as** + B | A 不如 B…… | He is **not as tall as** me. |
| **比较级 + and + 比较级** | 越来越…… | It's getting **colder and colder**. |
| **more and more** + 多音节 | 越来越…… | **more and more** beautiful |
| **The + 比较级..., the + 比较级...** | 越……就越…… | **The more** you learn, **the more** you know. 你学得越多，知道得就越多。 |
| **倍数 + as + 原级 + as** | 是……的几倍 | This room is **twice as big as** that one. |
| **倍数 + 比较级 + than** | 口语中可见，但可能产生歧义 | 更推荐 **three times as big as** |
| **the + 比较级 + of the two** | 两者中较……的 | He is **the taller of the two**. |

**使用时先确定比较对象：** `than` 后面必须是和前面同类、可比较的对象。比较人的身高、物的大小或动作的程度时，比较对象要保持一致。

- ✅ This book is **more useful than that one**.（书和书比较）
- ✅ She runs **faster than I do**.（她跑步的速度和我跑步的速度比较）
- ❌ She runs **faster than me run**.（`than` 后不能再直接接一个不完整的谓语结构）

**“越来越”和“越……越……”不是一回事：** `colder and colder` 表示变化持续加深；`the colder..., the ...` 表示两个变化相互关联。

**一句话小结：** 比较级先看比较对象是否一致，再看表达的是“更……”“越来越……”还是“越……越……”。

### ✍️ 练习 23.2：比较级句型

完成：This report is ____ than that one. (clear)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`clearer`

</details>

### 23.3 最高级句型

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| the + 最高级 + in + 范围（单数地点/群体） | He is **the tallest in** our class. |
| the + 最高级 + of + 范围（复数/of all） | He is **the tallest of** all the students. |
| one of the + 最高级 + 复数名词 | It's **one of the most beautiful cities**. |
| the + 序数词 + 最高级 | It's **the second largest** city. |

**⚠️ 最高级前必须加 the**（副词最高级可省略）。

### ✍️ 练习 23.3：最高级

翻译：这是最重要的邮件

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`This is the most important email.`

</details>

### 23.4 比较级的修饰语（高频考点）

**可以修饰比较级的词：**
`much, far, a lot, a great deal, even, still, a little, a bit, slightly, rather, no, any`

- He is **much taller** than me. 他比我高得多。
- He is **a little taller** than me. 他比我高一点。

**⚠️ 不能用 very / quite / too 修饰比较级！**
- ❌ very taller → ✅ **much** taller

**very 修饰原级，much 修饰比较级：**
- ✅ **very** tall / **much** taller

### ✍️ 练习 23.4：比较级修饰语

选择：much (better / best)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`much better`

</details>

### 23.5 比较级表示最高级含义（重点句型）

以下三句意思相同（他是班上最高的）：

1. He is **the tallest** student **in** our class.（最高级）
2. He is **taller than any other** student **in** our class.（比较级 + any other + 单数）
3. He is **taller than all the other** students **in** our class. 他比我们班其他所有学生都高。
4. He is **taller than anyone else** in our class. 他比我们班其他任何人都高。
5. **No one else** in our class is **as tall as** he. 我们班没有其他人和他一样高。
6. **No one else** in our class is **taller than** he. 我们班没有其他人比他高。

**⚠️ 注意：同一范围内用 `any other`，不同范围用 `any`：**
- China is larger than **any other country** in Asia.（中国在亚洲内）
- China is larger than **any country** in Africa.（中国不在非洲）

### ✍️ 练习 23.5：比较级表示最高级

翻译：没有人比她更细心

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`No one is more careful than her.`

</details>

### 23.6 比较对象要一致（高频错误）

比较的双方必须是**同类事物**。

- ❌ The weather in Beijing is colder than **Shanghai**.（天气 vs 城市）
- ✅ The weather in Beijing is colder than **that in** Shanghai. 北京的天气比上海的冷。
- ✅ The books here are newer than **those** in the library. 这里的书比图书馆里的新。

**记忆：** 单数用 `that`，复数用 `those`。

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **very 修饰比较级**：❌ very better → ✅ **much** better
2. **比较级前加 the**（除"the 比较级 of the two"和"the 比较级, the 比较级"）
3. **比较对象不一致**
4. **any other 漏掉 other**：
   - ❌ He is taller than any student in his class.（把自己也比了）
5. **双写规则**：❌ biger → ✅ big**g**er

### ✍️ 练习 23.6：比较对象一致

改正：The salary of this job is higher than that company

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`... higher than that of that company.`

</details>

## 24. 动词不定式入门

### 讲解

**不定式 = to + 动词原形**（有时省略 to）。它是**非谓语动词**的一种，可以充当名词、形容词、副词的角色。

### 24.1 不定式的形式

| 形式 | 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 一般式 | to do | I want **to go**. |
| 进行式 | to be doing | He seems **to be sleeping**. |
| 完成式 | to have done | He seems **to have finished**. |
| 被动式 | to be done | The work needs **to be done**. |
| 完成被动 | to have been done | It seems **to have been done**. |
| 否定式 | **not** to do | I told him **not to go**. |

### ✍️ 练习 24.1：不定式形式

补全：I want ____ (learn) English

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`to learn`

</details>

### 24.2 不定式的句法功能

| 功能 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 主语 | **To learn English** is important. |
| 主语（用 it 作形式主语，更常见） | **It** is important **to learn English**. |
| 宾语 | I want **to go** home. |
| 宾语（用 it 作形式宾语） | I find **it** hard **to learn English**. |
| 表语 | My dream is **to be a doctor**. |
| 定语（放名词后） | I have a lot of work **to do**. |
| 状语（目的） | I came here **to see you**. |
| 宾语补足语 | I want you **to go**. |

**理解不定式的三个常见意思：**

- 作名词成分：说明“做什么”这件事。`To exercise is healthy.`
- 作目的状语：说明“为什么做”。`I went out to buy some milk.`
- 作定语：说明“哪个、什么用途”。`I need a pen to write with.`

遇到 `to do` 时，不要只翻译成“去做”。先看它在句中回答的是“什么事”“为什么”还是“哪一个”。

**一句话小结：** 不定式既能表示动作本身，也能表示目的或对名词的补充说明。

### ✍️ 练习 24.2：不定式句法功能

判断：To read is useful. 中 `To read` 作主语。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 24.3 只能接不定式作宾语的动词（必背）

**口诀：一"两"三"四"想要 wish，"希望"计划 plan 要 decide，同意 agree 拒绝 refuse，设法 manage 假装 pretend**

| 动词 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| want | I **want to go**. 我想去。 |
| wish / hope / expect | I **hope to see** you. 我希望见到你。 |
| decide / determine | He **decided to leave**. 他决定离开。 |
| plan / intend | We **plan to travel**. 我们计划去旅行。 |
| agree / refuse | He **agreed to help**. 他同意帮忙。 |
| promise | She **promised to come**. 她答应会来。 |
| offer | He **offered to help**. 他主动提出帮忙。 |
| manage / fail | He **managed to finish** it. 他设法完成了它。 |
| pretend | He **pretended to be** asleep. 他假装睡着了。 |
| choose | He **chose to stay**. 他选择留下。 |
| afford | I can't **afford to buy** it. 我买不起它。 |
| learn | I **learned to swim**. 我学会了游泳。 |
| happen | I **happened to meet** him. 我碰巧遇见了他。 |
| seem / appear | He **seems to know** it. 他似乎知道这件事。 |
| would like / would love | I'**d like to go**. 我想去。 |

### ✍️ 练习 24.3：接不定式的动词

填空：She decided ____ (apply)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`to apply`

</details>

### 24.4 不带 to 的不定式（省 to）超高频

#### ① 使役动词：make / let / have

- He **made me cry**. 他把我弄哭了。
- She **let him go**. 她让他走了。
- I **had him repair** my car. 我让他修车。

**⚠️ 但被动语态中 to 要还原：**
- He **was made to cry**.（被动，要加 to）

#### ② 感官动词：see / hear / watch / feel / notice / observe / look at / listen to

| 结构 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| see sb. **do** sth. | 看见做了全过程 | I **saw him cross** the road. |
| see sb. **doing** sth. | 看见正在做 | I **saw him crossing** the road. |
| see sth. **done** | 看见被…… | I **saw the door opened**. |

**⚠️ 被动语态中 to 还原：**
- He **was seen to cross** the road. 有人看见他过马路。

#### ③ 情态动词后
- I **can swim**.

#### ④ 特殊结构

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| had better (not) do | You'**d better go** now. 你现在最好走。 |
| would rather do | I'**d rather stay**. |
| why not do | **Why not go** with us? 为什么不跟我们一起去呢？ |
| why do | **Why worry** about it? 何必为此担心呢？ |
| do nothing but do | He **did nothing but complain**. 他只会抱怨。 |
| cannot but do | I **cannot but agree**. 我不得不同意。 |

**⚠️ 但 but 前没有 do 时，but 后要加 to：**
- He has no choice **but to go**.（but 前是 choice，不是 do）
- He did nothing **but complain**.（but 前有 did）

### ✍️ 练习 24.4：省 to 不定式

改正：He made me to wait

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He made me wait.`

</details>

### 24.5 疑问词 + 不定式

**结构：** what/when/where/how/whether + to do（相当于名词从句）

- I don't know **what to do**. = I don't know what I should do. 我不知道该做什么。
- Can you tell me **how to get** there? 你能告诉我怎么去那里吗？
- He is thinking about **whether to go** or not. 他在考虑要不要去。

**⚠️ why 不能接不定式**（why not do 除外）

### ✍️ 练习 24.5：疑问词 + 不定式

翻译：我不知道该写什么

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I don't know what to write.`

</details>

### 24.6 不定式的重要句型

| 句型 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| too...to... | 太……而不能 | He is **too young to go** to school. |
| enough to | 足够……可以 | He is **old enough to drive**. |
| so...as to | 如此……以至于 | He was **so kind as to** help me. |
| in order to / so as to | 为了 | He got up early **in order to** catch the train. |
| only to do | 结果却（意外结果） | He hurried there, **only to find** nobody. |
| be about to do | 正要做 | I **was about to leave** when he came. |
| It takes sb. + 时间 + to do | 花某人多久做某事 | **It took me** two hours **to finish** it. |
| It is + adj. + for/of sb. + to do | 做某事对某人来说…… | **It is** hard **for me to** learn it. |

**⚠️ for 还是 of？**
- 形容词描述**事情**（important, hard, easy, necessary, possible, difficult）→ 用 **for**
  - It is important **for** us to learn English. 我们学英语很重要。
- 形容词描述**人的品质**（kind, nice, clever, foolish, careless, polite, rude, brave）→ 用 **of**
  - It is kind **of** you to help me. 你能帮我真是太好了。

**⚠️ so as to 不能放句首，in order to 可以：**
- ✅ **In order to** pass the exam, he studied hard. 为了通过考试，他努力学习。
- ❌ So as to pass the exam, ... 为了通过考试……

### ✍️ 练习 24.6：不定式句型

完成：It is important ____ (reply) promptly

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`to reply`

</details>

### 24.7 too...to 的特殊情况

- He is **too young to go** to school.（否定含义：太小不能上学）
- 但：`only too...to` = 非常……：I'm **only too glad to help** you.（非常乐意帮你）
- `too ready/eager/willing/anxious to` 也是肯定：He is **too ready to help** others.（他非常乐于助人）

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **使役/感官动词后加 to**：
   - ❌ He made me to cry. → ✅ He made me **cry**. 他把我弄哭了。
2. **被动语态忘记还原 to**：
   - ❌ He was made cry. → ✅ He was made **to cry**. 他被弄哭了。
3. **for / of 混用**
4. **不定式否定位置**：
   - ❌ to not go → ✅ **not to** go

### ✍️ 练习 24.7：too...to

翻译：文件太长，无法打印

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The file is too long to print.`

</details>

### ✍️ 追加练习 24：不定式综合

判断不定式的作用或完成句子：

1. I went to the library ____ (study)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`to study`（目的状语）

</details>
2. She has a lot of work ____ (do)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`to do`（定语）

</details>
3. It is important ____ (listen) carefully

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`to listen`

</details>
4. My plan is ____ (learn) ten new words every day

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`to learn`

</details>
5. 改错：`He wants going home early.`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He wants to go home early.`

</details>

## 25. 动名词入门

### 讲解

**动名词 = 动词 + ing**，具有**名词的性质**，可作主语、宾语、表语、定语。

### 25.1 动名词的形式

| 形式 | 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 一般式 | doing | **Swimming** is fun. |
| 被动式 | being done | He hates **being laughed at**. |
| 完成式 | having done | He denied **having stolen** it. |
| 否定式 | **not** doing | I regret **not going**. |

### ✍️ 练习 25.1：动名词形式

写出动名词：`read`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`reading`。

</details>

### 25.2 动名词的句法功能

| 功能 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 主语 | **Swimming** is good exercise. |
| 宾语 | I enjoy **reading**. |
| 介词宾语 | He is good at **swimming**. |
| 表语 | My hobby is **reading**. |
| 定语（表用途） | a **swimming** pool（游泳池）= a pool for swimming |

**⚠️ 动名词作定语 vs 现在分词作定语：**
- a **sleeping** car = 卧铺车厢（动名词，表用途 = a car for sleeping）
- a **sleeping** baby = 熟睡的婴儿（现在分词，表状态 = a baby who is sleeping）

### ✍️ 练习 25.2：动名词句法功能

判断：Reading helps me relax. 中 `Reading` 作主语。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 25.3 只能接动名词作宾语的动词（必背）

**口诀：建议 suggest、完成 finish、盼望 mind、原谅 excuse，避免 avoid、错过 miss，练习 practice、否认 deny，喜欢 enjoy、想象 imagine，允许 allow、承认 admit，考虑 consider、推迟 delay，冒险 risk、忍受 stand**

| 动词 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| enjoy | I **enjoy reading**. |
| finish | He **finished writing**. |
| mind | Would you **mind opening** the window? 你介意开一下窗户吗？ |
| suggest | He **suggested going** by bus. 他建议坐公交车去。 |
| avoid | He **avoided answering**. |
| practice | She **practices playing** the piano. 她练习弹钢琴。 |
| keep (on) | **Keep working**! |
| consider | I'm **considering moving**. |
| imagine | I can't **imagine living** there. 我无法想象住在那里。 |
| admit / deny | He **denied stealing** it. 他否认偷了它。 |
| miss | I **missed seeing** the film. 我错过了看这部电影。 |
| escape | He **escaped being punished**. 他逃脱了惩罚。 |
| risk | Don't **risk losing** your job. 别冒险丢掉你的工作。 |
| delay / put off | Don't **put off doing** it. 别把它拖延下去。 |
| give up | He **gave up smoking**. 他戒烟了。 |
| can't help | I **can't help laughing**. 我忍不住笑。 |
| feel like | I **feel like eating** something. 我想吃点东西。 |
| be worth | The book is **worth reading**. 这本书值得一读。 |
| It's no use / no good | **It's no use crying**. 哭也没用。 |
| have difficulty/trouble (in) | I have difficulty **learning** it. 我学这个有困难。 |
| spend time (in) | He spends much time **playing**. 他花很多时间玩耍。 |
| be busy | He is busy **working**. 他正忙着工作。 |
| look forward to | I'm looking forward to **seeing** you. 我期待见到你。 |
| be/get used to | I'm used to **getting** up early. 我已经习惯早起了。 |
| pay attention to | Pay attention to **listening**. 要注意听。 |
| devote to | He devoted himself to **teaching**. 他献身于教学。 |
| object to | I object to **going** there. 我反对去那里。 |
| prefer A to B | I prefer **swimming** to **running**. 我更喜欢游泳而不是跑步。 |

### ✍️ 练习 25.3：接动名词的动词

填空：We finished ____ (discuss) the plan

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`discussing`

</details>

### 25.4 既可接不定式也可接动名词（含义相同）

`begin, start, continue, like, love, hate, prefer, intend, attempt`

- I like **swimming**. = I like **to swim**. 我喜欢游泳。

**⚠️ 但以下情况只能用不定式：**
1. 主语是**物**：The engine began **to work**.
2. 前面动词是**进行时**：It's beginning **to rain**.
3. 后面是**状态动词**（know, understand）：I began **to understand**.

### ✍️ 练习 25.4：不定式或动名词

选择：I like (to read / reading) before bed

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：两者通常都可以。

</details>

### 25.5 接不定式和动名词含义不同的动词（超高频考点）

| 动词 | + doing | + to do |
|---|---|---|
| **remember** | 记得**做过**（过去） | 记得**要做**（将来） |
| | I **remember locking** the door. 我记得锁过门。 | **Remember to lock** the door. 记得锁门。 |
| **forget** | 忘记**做过** | 忘记**要做** |
| | I **forgot posting** the letter. 我忘了寄过信。 | I **forgot to post** the letter. 我忘记寄信了。 |
| **regret** | 后悔**做过** | 遗憾**要说/告知** |
| | I **regret telling** him. 我后悔告诉他了。 | I **regret to tell** you... 很遗憾地告诉你…… |
| **stop** | **停止**做某事 | 停下来**去做**另一件事 |
| | He **stopped smoking**. 他戒烟了。 | He **stopped to smoke**. 他停下来抽烟。 |
| **try** | **试着**做（尝试新方法） | **努力/设法**去做 |
| | **Try knocking** the door. 试试敲门。 | **Try to finish** it. 尽力完成它。 |
| **mean** | 意味着 | 打算 |
| | It **means working** overtime. 这意味着加班。 | I **mean to help** you. 我打算帮你。 |
| **go on** | 继续做**同一件事** | 接着做**另一件事** |
| | **Go on reading**. 继续读。 | **Go on to read** the next. 接着读下一篇。 |
| **can't help** | 忍不住做 | 不能帮忙做 |
| | I **can't help laughing**. 忍不住笑。 | I **can't help to do** it. 我帮不了。 |

**选择 `doing` 还是 `to do` 的实用方法：** 先看前面的动词是否固定搭配；如果两种形式都可以，再判断含义是否改变。`remember/forget/regret/stop/try` 后面两种形式常常不是简单替换，而是改变时间或动作关系。

例如：

- `I remember **locking** the door.` 回忆已经做过的事。
- `Remember **to lock** the door.` 提醒将要做的事。
- `He stopped **smoking**.` 停止原来的动作。
- `He stopped **to smoke**.` 停下来，转而去做另一件事。

**一句话小结：** `doing` 常把动作当作经历、活动或正在进行的对象；`to do` 常指计划、目的或将要做的动作，但最终要以固定搭配和上下文为准。

### ✍️ 练习 25.5：含义不同的搭配

选择：I stopped (to talk / talking) because the meeting began

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`talking`

</details>

### 25.6 need / want / require / deserve + doing（主动表被动）

**这些动词后接动名词时，用主动形式表示被动含义：**

- The car **needs washing**. 这辆车需要清洗。 = The car **needs to be washed**. 车需要洗。
- The room **wants cleaning**. = The room **wants to be cleaned**. 这个房间需要打扫。
- This problem **requires solving**. = ...**requires to be solved**. 这个问题需要解决。

### ✍️ 练习 25.6：动名词主动表被动

改写：The report needs to be checked

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The report needs checking.`

</details>

### 25.7 动名词的复合结构

**结构：物主代词/名词所有格 + 动名词**

- **His coming** made us happy. 他的到来让我们开心。
- Do you mind **my opening** the window? 你介意我开窗吗？
- I appreciate **your helping** me. 感谢你帮我。

**口语中可用宾格：** Do you mind **me** opening the window?

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **介词后用动词原形**：
   - ❌ He is good at swim. → ✅ good at **swimming** 擅长游泳
2. **look forward to 后用原形**：
   - ❌ look forward to see you → ✅ look forward to **seeing** you
3. **stop doing / stop to do 混用**
4. **remember doing / to do 混用**
5. **只接动名词的动词后用了不定式**：
   - ❌ I enjoy to read. → ✅ I enjoy **reading**. 我喜欢阅读。

<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`your`

</details>

## 🌐 第二阶段综合任务：讲述经历并安排计划

> **任务目标：** 按时间顺序讲清一次过去经历，并说明接下来的计划。

### 1. 听力：一场改变的旅行

请让学习伙伴朗读下文，或用文本转语音工具播放两遍。第一遍只判断事情发生的先后，第二遍记录过去时和将来表达。

> Last Saturday, Leo planned to visit Oxford. While he was waiting for the train, he received a message from his sister. She needed help moving to a new apartment, so Leo changed his plan. He helped her all afternoon. They are going to visit Oxford together next month.
>
> 上周六，利奥原计划去牛津。他等火车时收到了姐姐的消息。姐姐搬新公寓需要帮助，所以利奥改变了计划。他整个下午都在帮她。他们打算下个月一起去牛津。

听后回答：What was Leo doing when he received the message?（利奥收到消息时正在做什么？）Why did he change his plan?（他为什么改变计划？）What are they going to do next month?（他们下个月打算做什么？）

### 2. 阅读：制作时间线

把故事整理为 `plan → interruption → decision → future arrangement` 四个节点，并说明 `planned`、`was waiting` 和 `are going to visit` 为什么使用不同的时态。

### 3. 口语：讲述计划被打断的一天

准备一分钟后，连续讲述1至2分钟。至少使用：

- 两个一般过去时动作
- 一个过去进行时背景
- 一个 `because` 或 `so` 表达原因或结果
- 一个 `will` 或 `be going to` 表达后续计划

### 4. 写作：给朋友写邮件

写一封80至100词的非正式邮件，说明原计划、发生的变化、你的感受和新安排。使用恰当的称呼、分段和结束语。

**参考结构：** `I planned to...` → `While I was..., ...` → `So I decided to...` → `Next week, I am going to...`

**达标标准：** 能正确还原4个时间节点；口语包含完整的过去和未来关系；邮件达到80词且分段清楚；时态、情态动词和非谓语形式正确率达到80%。

---
---

# 第三阶段 · 进阶语法

> **本阶段目标：** 能表达动作与现在的联系、先后关系、被动关系，并读懂三个主要类型的从句。
>
> **学习提醒：** 学从句时先找主句的主语和谓语，再判断从句在整句中充当什么成分。

---

## 26. 现在完成时

### 讲解

> **先理解：** 现在完成时不是普通的过去时。它强调过去的动作和现在仍有联系，通常不和明确的过去时间连用。

现在完成时是中国学生**最难理解**的时态，因为中文没有对应。

**核心概念：** 现在完成时讲的**不是过去**，而是**过去的动作对现在造成的影响或结果**。它的落脚点始终在**现在**。

**先问自己一个问题：我是否需要说明具体的过去时间？** 如果需要，通常用一般过去时；如果不说具体时间，只强调经历、结果或持续到现在的状态，通常考虑现在完成时。

### 26.1 结构

**核心公式：have / has + 过去分词（p.p.）**

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 肯定 | I **have finished** my work. |
| 否定 | I **haven't finished** my work. |
| 疑问 | **Have** you **finished**? |
| 简答 | Yes, I **have**. / No, I **haven't**. |

主语是三单 → 用 `has`；其他 → 用 `have`。

### ✍️ 练习 26.1：现在完成时结构

填空：I ____ ____ (send) the email

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`have sent`

</details>

### 26.2 两大用法

#### 用法一：过去的动作对现在的影响（"完成用法"）

强调**结果**，不强调什么时候做的。

- I **have lost** my key.（结果：现在还没找到）
- He **has gone** to Beijing.（结果：他现在不在这儿）
- She **has cleaned** the room.（结果：房间现在很干净）

**现在完成时和一般过去时的对比：**

- I **have lost** my key.（现在还没找到，强调结果。）
- I **lost** my key yesterday.（昨天丢的，说明具体过去时间。）
- **Have** you **seen** this film?（问有没有这种经历。）
- **Did** you **see** this film last night?（问昨晚这个具体时间发生的事。）

不能说：`I have seen him yesterday.` 因为 `yesterday` 已经把时间明确放在过去，应改为 `I saw him yesterday.`

**常用时间词：** `already, yet, just, ever, never, before, recently, lately, so far, up to now, in the past few years, this week/month/year`

| 词 | 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| already | 肯定句"已经" | I have **already** finished. |
| yet | 否定句"还没"/疑问句"已经" | I haven't finished **yet**. / Have you finished **yet**? |
| just | "刚刚" | He has **just** left. |
| ever | 疑问句"曾经" | Have you **ever** been there? |
| never | "从未" | I have **never** seen it. |

#### 用法二：从过去持续到现在的动作或状态（"持续用法"）

强调**持续**，动作可能还会继续。

- I **have lived** here **for** ten years. 我在这儿住了十年了（现在还住着）。
- He **has been** ill **since** Monday. 他从周一病到现在。

**⚠️ 只能用延续性动词！**

### ✍️ 练习 26.2：现在完成时用法

选择：She has lived here (for / since) 2020

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`since`

</details>

### 26.3 for 与 since（超高频考点）

| 词 | 后接 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **for** | 时间**段** | **for** three years, **for** a long time, **for** two hours |
| **since** | 时间**点**或从句 | **since** 2010, **since** last week, **since** I came here |

- I have worked here **for** five years. 我在这里工作五年了。
- I have worked here **since** 2019. 我从2019年起就在这里工作了。
- I have worked here **since** I graduated. 我毕业以后就一直在这里工作。

**since 的特殊句型：**
- It is / has been + 时间段 + since + 过去时从句
  - **It is** five years **since** I came here. 我来这儿五年了。

### ✍️ 练习 26.3：for 与 since

填空：We have worked together ____ five years

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`for`

</details>

### 26.4 延续性动词 vs 非延续性动词（超高频难点）

**非延续性动词（短暂动词）不能与 for/since/how long 连用！**

| 非延续性（❌ 不能跟 for/since） | 延续性（✅ 可以） | 含义 |
|---|---|---|
| come / go / arrive | be here / be there / be in | 来/去/到 |
| buy | have | 买 → 拥有 |
| borrow | keep | 借 → 保留 |
| lend | keep | 借出 → 保留 |
| leave | be away (from) | 离开 → 不在 |
| die | be dead | 死 → 死了（状态） |
| finish / end | be over | 结束 |
| begin / start | be on | 开始 → 进行中 |
| join | be in / be a member of | 加入 → 是成员 |
| marry | be married | 结婚 → 已婚 |
| get up | be up | 起床 |
| fall asleep | be asleep | 入睡 |
| catch a cold | have a cold | 感冒 |
| open | be open | 打开 |
| close | be closed | 关上 |
| put on | wear / have on | 穿上 → 穿着 |
| know（becomes）| know | 知道（本身是延续性） |

**改写示例：**
- ❌ He has **died** for three years. 他死了三年了。
- ✅ He **has been dead** for three years. 他死了三年了。
- ✅ He **died** three years ago.（用一般过去时）

- ❌ I have **bought** this book for a week. 我买这本书一周了。
- ✅ I **have had** this book for a week. 我有这本书一周了。

- ❌ He has **joined** the army for two years. 他参军两年了。
- ✅ He **has been in** the army for two years. 他在部队待了两年了。

### ✍️ 练习 26.4：延续性动词

改写：He left two hours ago

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He has been away for two hours.`

</details>

### 26.5 have been to / have gone to / have been in（经典对比）

| 结构 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **have been to** | 去过（**已回来**） | He **has been to** Beijing. 他去过北京。 |
| **have gone to** | 去了（**还没回来**） | He **has gone to** Beijing. 他去北京了（人不在）。 |
| **have been in** | 待在某地（**强调时长**） | He **has been in** Beijing for 3 years. |

### ✍️ 练习 26.5：been to / gone to / been in

选择：Tom isn't here. He has (been to / gone to) the bank

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`gone to`

</details>

### 26.6 现在完成时 vs 一般过去时（核心区别）

| 对比 | 一般过去时 | 现在完成时 |
|---|---|---|
| 焦点 | **过去**发生的事 | 对**现在**的影响 |
| 时间状语 | 有明确过去时间（yesterday, last week, in 2010, ago） | 不能有明确过去时间 |
| 例句 | I **lost** my key yesterday. | I **have lost** my key.（现在没钥匙） |

**⚠️ 铁律：有明确的过去时间状语，就不能用现在完成时！**
- ❌ I have seen him yesterday. 我昨天见过他。
- ✅ I **saw** him yesterday. 我昨天见过他。

**⚠️ 但 when 提问一定用过去时：**
- ❌ When have you finished it? 你什么时候完成的？
- ✅ **When did** you finish it? 你什么时候完成的？

### ✍️ 练习 26.6：现在完成时与过去时

选择：I (saw / have seen) him yesterday

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`saw`

</details>

### 26.7 This is the first time + 完成时

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| This is the first/second time that + 现在完成时 | This is the first time (that) I **have been** here. |
| That was the first time that + 过去完成时 | That was the first time (that) I **had been** there. |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **非延续性动词 + for/since**（最高频）
2. **有明确过去时间还用完成时**
3. **have been to / have gone to 混用**
4. **过去式当过去分词用**：❌ I have went → ✅ I have **gone**
5. **since 后接时间段**：❌ since three years → ✅ **for** three years

### ✍️ 练习 26.7：This is the first time

完成：This is the first time I ____ (use) this tool

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`have used`

</details>

<details>

</details>

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`has; left`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`have not finished`

4. 翻译：你去过日本吗？

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Have you ever been to Japan?`

</details>
5. 改错：`He has gone to Beijing last week.`

<details>

答案：`He went to Beijing last week.`

## 27. 过去完成时

### 讲解

**核心概念：过去的过去。** 表示在过去某个时间点**之前**已经完成的动作。

**时间轴：**
```
过去的过去          过去某时          现在
    |                 |                |
过去完成时        一般过去时        现在时
had done            did             do
```

### 27.1 结构

**核心公式：had + 过去分词**（所有人称都用 had）

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 肯定 | He **had finished** it before I came. |
| 否定 | He **hadn't finished** it. |
| 一般疑问 | **Had** he **finished** it? |
| 特殊疑问 | What **had** he **finished**? |
| 简答 | Yes, he **had**. / No, he **hadn't**. |

### ✍️ 练习 27.1：过去完成时结构

填空：The meeting ____ already ____ (start) when I arrived

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`had already started`

</details>

### 27.2 使用条件（必须有"过去的参照点"）

过去完成时通常需要一个**明确说出或由上下文可知的过去参照点**，用来说明某动作在那个过去时刻之前已经发生。

| 参照方式 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| by + 过去时间 | **By** the end of last year, he **had learned** 3000 words. |
| before + 过去时从句 | He **had left before** I **arrived**. |
| when + 过去时从句 | When I **got** there, he **had gone**. |
| after + 过去完成时 | **After** he **had finished** the work, he went home. |
| 宾语从句（主句过去时） | He said he **had seen** the film. |

### ✍️ 练习 27.2：过去完成时参照点

判断：先发生的动作使用 `had + done`。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 27.3 before / after 的特殊性

用 `before` 和 `after` 时，**时间先后已经很清楚**，所以两个动作都可以用一般过去时：

- He **had left** before I arrived. = He **left** before I arrived.（都对）

### ✍️ 练习 27.3：before / after

完成：After she ____ (finish), she left

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`had finished`

</details>

### 27.4 表示"未实现的愿望"（重要句型）

以下动词用过去完成时表示"**本想……但没实现**"：

`hope, want, expect, intend, plan, mean, think, suppose`

- I **had hoped** to see you.（本希望见你，但没见成）
- We **had planned** to leave earlier.（本打算早点走）

**也可用：** 过去式 + 完成式不定式
- I **hoped to have seen** you. 我本希望已经见到你了。
- I **intended to have told** you. 我本打算已经告诉你了。

### ✍️ 练习 27.4：未实现的愿望

翻译：我真希望我当时听了你的话

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I wish I had listened to you.`

</details>

### 27.5 固定句型

| 句型 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| No sooner had...than... | 一……就…… | **No sooner had** he arrived **than** it began to rain. |
| Hardly/Scarcely had...when... | 一……就…… | **Hardly had** he sat down **when** the phone rang. |
| It was the first time that + 过去完成时 | 那是第一次…… | It **was** the first time that I **had seen** it. |

**⚠️ No sooner / Hardly 放句首时，主句要**部分倒装**。

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **单独使用过去完成时**（没有过去的参照点）：
   - ❌ I had gone to school yesterday. → ✅ I **went** to school yesterday. 我昨天去上学了。
2. **主从句时态搞反**：
   - ✅ 先发生的用过去完成时，后发生的用一般过去时
3. **had + 过去式**：❌ had went → ✅ had **gone**

### ✍️ 练习 27.5：过去完成时固定句型

填空：It was the first time he ____ (visit) the city

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`had visited`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 27

用适当时态填空：

1. When I ___ (arrive), the train ___ (leave). 我到的时候，火车已经开走了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：arrived / had left

</details>
2. He said he ___ (finish) the work. 他说他已经完成了工作。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：had finished

</details>
3. By the end of last year, she ___ (learn) 2000 English words. 到去年年底，她已经学了2000个英语单词。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：had learned

</details>
4. After he ___ (have) breakfast, he went to work. 他吃完早饭后就去上班了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：had had

</details>
5. I ___ (hope) to see you, but you were out.（本希望）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：had hoped

</details>

改错：

6. ❌ I had finished my homework yesterday. 我昨天做完了作业。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ I **finished** my homework yesterday.

</details>
7. ❌ He told me he has seen the film. 他告诉我他看过这部电影。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ He told me he **had seen** the film.

</details>
8. ❌ When I got there, he had went. 我到那里的时候，他已经走了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ When I got there, he had **gone**.

</details>

## 28. 将来完成时

### 讲解

**核心概念：** 表示在**将来某个时间之前**将会完成的动作。

### 28.1 结构

**核心公式：will have + 过去分词**

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 肯定 | I **will have finished** it by tomorrow. |
| 否定 | I **won't have finished** it. |
| 一般疑问 | **Will** you **have finished** it? |
| 简答 | Yes, I **will**. / No, I **won't**. |

### ✍️ 练习 28.1：将来完成时结构

填空：By tomorrow, she ____ ____ ____ (finish) the report

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`will have finished`

</details>

### 28.2 标志词

`by + 将来时间`（by tomorrow, by next week, by the end of this year）
`by the time + 一般现在时从句`（主将从现）

- **By** next month, I **will have worked** here for 5 years. 到下个月，我在这里就工作满五年了。
- **By the time** you **arrive**, we **will have finished** dinner. 等你到的时候，我们就已经吃完晚饭了。

### ✍️ 练习 28.2：将来完成时标志词

选择：By next Friday, they (will complete / will have completed) the project

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`will have completed`

</details>

### 28.3 by 引导的时态判断表（超高频考点）

| by + 时间 | 用什么时态 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| by + **过去**时间 | 过去完成时 | **By** 2010, he **had graduated**. |
| by + **将来**时间 | 将来完成时 | **By** 2030, he **will have retired**. |
| by **now** / by **this time** | 现在完成时 | **By now** he **has finished**. |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **by the time 从句用将来时**：
   - ❌ By the time you will arrive... → ✅ By the time you **arrive**... 等你到的时候……
2. **by 后时间和时态不匹配**

### ✍️ 练习 28.3：by 引导的判断

翻译：到明年六月，我将在这里工作五年

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`By next June, I will have worked here for five years.`

</details>

1. By Friday, we ____ ____ (finish) the report

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`will have finished`

</details>

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`will have worked`

</details>
3. By the time the guests arrive, I ____ ____ (prepare) dinner

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`will have prepared`

</details>
4. 选择：By 2035, he (will retire / will have retired) from the company

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`will have retired`

</details>
5. 改错：`By the time you will come, I will have left.`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`By the time you come, I will have left.`

填空：

1. By the end of this year, I ___ (work) here for ten years. 到今年年底，我在这里就工作满十年了。

<details>

</details>

## 29. 完成进行时

### 讲解

**核心概念：强调动作从过去持续到现在（或某时），并且还在继续。** 比完成时更强调**过程和持续**。

### 29.1 三种完成进行时

| 时态 | 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 现在完成进行时 | **have/has been + doing** | I **have been waiting** for two hours. 我已经等了两小时了（还在等）。 |
| 过去完成进行时 | **had been + doing** | He **had been working** there for 5 years before he left. |
| 将来完成进行时 | **will have been + doing** | By next year, I **will have been living** here for 10 years. |

**现在完成进行时的完整句型：**

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 肯定 | I **have been waiting** for two hours. 我已经等了两个小时了。 |
| 否定 | I **haven't been waiting** long. 我没等多久。 |
| 一般疑问 | **Have** you **been waiting** long? 你等很久了吗？ |
| 特殊疑问 | How long **have** you **been waiting**? 你等了多久了？ |
| 简答 | Yes, I **have**. / No, I **haven't**. |

### ✍️ 练习 29.1：完成进行时

选择：I (have read / have been reading) for two hours

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`have been reading`

</details>

### 29.2 现在完成时 vs 现在完成进行时

| 对比 | 现在完成时 | 现在完成进行时 |
|---|---|---|
| 强调 | **结果**、完成 | **过程**、持续 |
| 例句 | I **have painted** the room.（房间刷完了） | I **have been painting** the room.（一直在刷，可能还没完） |
| 例句 | I **have read** the book.（读完了） | I **have been reading** the book.（一直在读） |

**很多情况下两者可互换**（尤其是 live, work, study, teach 等延续性动词）：
- I **have lived** here for 10 years. = I **have been living** here for 10 years. 我在这里住了十年了。

### ✍️ 练习 29.2：完成时与完成进行时

完成：She has ____ three reports today. (write)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`written`

</details>

### 29.3 不用于完成进行时的动词

**状态动词**（know, believe, like, have, be, own, belong）不用进行时，因此也不用完成进行时：
- ❌ I have been knowing him for years. 我认识他很多年了。
- ✅ I **have known** him for years. 我认识他很多年了。

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **状态动词误用完成进行时**
2. **结构写错**：❌ have being doing → ✅ have **been** doing

### ✍️ 练习 29.3：不用于完成进行时的动词

改正：I have been knowing him for years

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I have known him for years.`

</details>

### ✍️ 追加练习 29：完成时与完成进行时对比

选择或改错：

1. I ____ (have read / have been reading) this book all afternoon

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`have been reading`

</details>
2. She ____ three emails so far。 (has written / has been writing)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`has written`

</details>
3. We ____ (have known / have been knowing) each other since childhood

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`have known`

</details>
4. 翻译：他已经工作了三个小时，现在还在工作

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He has been working for three hours, and he is still working.`

</details>
5. 改错：`They have been owning this house for ten years.`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`They have owned this house for ten years.`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 29

填空：

1. I ___ (wait) for you for two hours! 我等你两个小时了！

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：have been waiting

</details>
2. He ___ (study) English since 2015. 他从2015年起就一直在学英语。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：has been studying（has studied）

</details>
3. She ___ (work) here for 5 years before she left. 她离开之前已经在这里工作了五年。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：had been working

</details>
4. I ___ (know) him for ten years.（注意状态动词）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：have known

</details>

## 30. 16 种时态总表

### 讲解

英语时态 = **时间（4 种）× 状态（4 种）= 16 种**。

**四种时间：** 现在、过去、将来、过去将来
**四种状态：** 一般、进行、完成、完成进行

### 30.1 完整时态表（以 do 为例，主语 he）

| | **一般** | **进行** | **完成** | **完成进行** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **现在** | does | is doing | has done | has been doing |
| **过去** | did | was doing | had done | had been doing |
| **将来** | will do | will be doing | will have done | will have been doing |
| **过去将来** | would do | would be doing | would have done | would have been doing |

### ✍️ 练习 30.1：完整时态表

写出 he / work 的现在完成时

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He has worked.`

</details>

### 30.2 常用 8 种时态速查（初中高中必考）

| 时态 | 结构 | 标志词 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 一般现在时 | do / does | every day, usually, often | He **works** here. |
| 一般过去时 | did | yesterday, ago, last | He **worked** here. |
| 一般将来时 | will do / be going to do | tomorrow, next | He **will work** here. |
| 现在进行时 | am/is/are doing | now, Look!, Listen! | He **is working**. |
| 过去进行时 | was/were doing | at 8 last night, while | He **was working**. |
| 现在完成时 | have/has done | already, yet, just, for, since, ever | He **has worked** here for 5 years. |
| 过去完成时 | had done | by + 过去时间, before, after | He **had worked** here before 2010. |
| 过去将来时 | would do | 主句过去时的宾语从句 | He said he **would work** here. |

### ✍️ 练习 30.2：常用时态

选择：She ____ email every morning. (checks / is checking)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`checks`

</details>

### 30.3 时态选择决策树

```
问自己 3 个问题：

1️⃣ 动作发生在什么时间？
   现在/经常 → 现在时
   过去 → 过去时
   将来 → 将来时
   从过去看将来 → 过去将来时

2️⃣ 动作是什么状态？
   单纯陈述 → 一般
   正在进行 → 进行
   已完成/有影响 → 完成
   持续进行且强调过程 → 完成进行

3️⃣ 有没有特殊规则？
   主将从现？
   非延续性动词？
   状态动词不用进行时？
```

判断：Look! 表示现在进行时

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

</details>

### 30.4 时态判断经典练习

| 中文 | 正确时态 | 英文 |
|---|---|---|
| 我每天跑步。 | 一般现在 | I **run** every day. |
| 我昨天跑了步。 | 一般过去 | I **ran** yesterday. |
| 我明天要跑步。 | 一般将来 | I **will run** tomorrow. |
| 我正在跑步。 | 现在进行 | I **am running**. |
| 昨天这时我正在跑步。 | 过去进行 | I **was running** at this time yesterday. |
| 我已经跑完了。 | 现在完成 | I **have run**. |
| 我跑了两小时了。 | 现在完成进行 | I **have been running** for 2 hours. |
| 他来之前我已经跑完了。 | 过去完成 | I **had run** before he came. |
| 到明天我就跑完了。 | 将来完成 | I **will have run** by tomorrow. |

## 31. 被动语态

### 讲解

**主动语态：** 主语是动作的**执行者**（I wrote the letter.）
**被动语态：** 主语是动作的**承受者**（The letter **was written** by me.）

### 31.1 被动语态的构成

**核心公式：be + 过去分词（+ by + 动作执行者）**

**什么时候优先用被动语态？**

1. 重点是动作承受者：`The window was broken.`（窗户被打破了。）
2. 执行者未知：`My bike was stolen.`（不知道是谁偷的。）
3. 执行者不重要或显而易见：`English is spoken here.`
4. 说明流程或正式事实：`The form must be completed before Friday.`

如果执行者很重要，保留 `by` 短语；如果重点是“谁做的”，主动语态通常更自然：`The manager approved the plan.`

**变被动时只改变视角，不改变时态：** 先找主动句的宾语，再保留原来的时态，把动词改成 `be + 过去分词`。不要因为看到过去分词就误以为一定是过去时。

**一句话小结：** 主动语态突出谁做，被动语态突出谁或什么承受动作；时态由 `be` 的形式体现。

### ✍️ 练习 31.1：被动语态构成

改写：The team completed the report

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The report was completed by the team.`

</details>

### 31.2 各时态的被动语态（重点表）

| 时态 | 主动 | 被动 |
|---|---|---|
| 一般现在 | He **writes** it. | It **is written**. |
| 一般过去 | He **wrote** it. | It **was written**. |
| 一般将来 | He **will write** it. | It **will be written**. |
| 过去将来 | He **would write** it. | It **would be written**. |
| 现在进行 | He **is writing** it. | It **is being written**. |
| 过去进行 | He **was writing** it. | It **was being written**. |
| 现在完成 | He **has written** it. | It **has been written**. |
| 过去完成 | He **had written** it. | It **had been written**. |
| 将来完成 | He **will have written** it. | It **will have been written**. |
| 情态动词 | He **can write** it. | It **can be written**. |
| 不定式 | to write | to **be written** |
| 动名词 | writing | **being written** |

**⚠️ 进行时被动 = be + being + done**（没有 will be being done）

### ✍️ 练习 31.2：各时态被动

填空：The email ____ ____ (send) yesterday

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`was sent`

</details>

### 31.3 主动变被动的步骤

```
He      wrote     the letter    yesterday.
主语     谓语        宾语          状语
 ↓                   ↓
by him ←         主语（提到前面）

The letter   was written   by him   yesterday.
   主语        be+p.p.     by+宾格    状语
```

**三步走：**
1. 把宾语变主语
2. 谓语变 `be + 过去分词`（**时态不变**，be 的形式和新主语一致）
3. 原主语变 `by + 宾格`（可省略）

### ✍️ 练习 31.3：主动变被动

判断被动句的主语：The file was opened by Anna

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The file`

</details>

### 31.4 双宾语的被动（两种变法）

`He gave me a book.`
1. **I was given a book** by him.（间接宾语作主语，更常用）
2. **A book was given to me** by him.（直接宾语作主语，注意加 to）

### ✍️ 练习 31.4：双宾语被动

改写：They gave me a task

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I was given a task.`

</details>

### 31.5 带宾补的被动

`We call him Tom.` → **He is called Tom**.
`They made him work.` → **He was made to work**.（注意 to 还原！）

### ✍️ 练习 31.5：带宾补的被动

改写：They elected him chair

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He was elected chair.`

</details>

### 31.6 不能用被动语态的情况

| 情况 | 例子 |
|---|---|
| 不及物动词 | happen, take place, break out, appear, disappear, belong to, last, remain, rise |
| 系动词 | be, look, seem, become, taste, sound |
| 表状态的及物动词 | have(有), cost, fit, suit, lack, resemble, mean |
| 部分固定短语 | take place, break out, come true, run out |

- ❌ The accident **was happened** yesterday. 事故昨天发生了。
- ✅ The accident **happened** yesterday. 事故昨天发生了。
- ❌ The war was broken out in 1939. 战争于1939年爆发。
- ✅ The war **broke out** in 1939. 战争于1939年爆发。

### ✍️ 练习 31.6：不能被动的动词

判断：The accident happened. 不能改为 `was happened`。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 31.7 主动形式表被动含义（重点）

| 情况 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| need/want/require/deserve + doing | The car **needs washing**. 这辆车需要清洗。 |
| 系动词 + 形容词 | The book **sells well**. 这书卖得好。 |
| | The pen **writes** smoothly. 这支笔写起来很顺畅。 |
| | The cloth **feels** soft. 这块布摸起来很软。 |
| | The food **tastes** good. 这食物尝起来很好吃。 |
| | The door **won't open**. 这扇门打不开。 |
| be worth doing | The film is **worth seeing**. 这部电影值得一看。 |
| 不定式作定语（有些） | I have a lot of work **to do**. |
| too...to 结构 | The box is too heavy **to carry**. |

### ✍️ 练习 31.7：主动形式表被动

选择：This book (sells / is sold) well

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`sells`

</details>

### 31.8 常见被动句型（It 引导，书面常用）

| 句型 | 含义 |
|---|---|
| It is said that... | 据说 |
| It is reported that... | 据报道 |
| It is believed that... | 人们相信 |
| It is thought that... | 人们认为 |
| It is known that... | 众所周知 |
| It is hoped that... | 希望 |
| It is suggested that... | 有人建议 |
| It is well known that... | 众所周知 |

**可转换为：** `Sb./Sth. is said to do / to have done...`
- It is said that he is a doctor. = He **is said to be** a doctor. 据说他是一名医生。
- It is said that he was a doctor. = He **is said to have been** a doctor. 据说他曾经是一名医生。

### ✍️ 练习 31.8：It 引导的被动

翻译：据说他很可靠

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`It is said that he is reliable.`

</details>

### 31.9 get + 过去分词（口语被动）

- He **got hurt** in the accident. 他在事故中受伤了。
- They **got married** last year. 他们去年结婚了。
- My phone **got stolen**. 我的手机被偷了。

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **不及物动词用被动**：❌ was happened → ✅ happened
2. **忘记 be 动词**：❌ The letter written by him. → ✅ The letter **was** written.
3. **时态变了**：主动是过去时，被动也必须是过去时
4. **使役/感官动词被动忘记加 to**：❌ He was made work. → ✅ He was made **to** work.
5. **by 后用主格**：❌ by he → ✅ by **him**

翻译：小心别被雨淋湿

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Be careful not to get caught in the rain.`

</details>

## 32. 定语从句

### 讲解

> **先理解：** 定语从句就是“放在名词后面的说明”。先找到被说明的名词，再看关系词后面的信息。

**定语从句 = 用一个句子来修饰名词**（相当于一个"超长形容词"）。

**结构：** 先行词 + 关系词 + 从句

```
The man   who is standing there   is my teacher.
 先行词        定语从句              主句谓语
```

### 32.1 关系代词与关系副词

| 关系词 | 指代 | 在从句中作 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **who** | 人 | 主语/宾语 | The man **who** called me is Tom. |
| **whom** | 人 | 宾语（正式） | The man **whom** I met is Tom. |
| **whose** | 人/物 | 定语 | The boy **whose** father is a doctor... |
| **which** | 物 | 主语/宾语 | The book **which** I bought is nice. |
| **that** | 人/物 | 主语/宾语 | The book **that** I bought... |
| **as** | 人/物 | 主语/宾语 | Such a book **as** you need... |
| **when** | 时间 | 状语 | I remember the day **when** we met. |
| **where** | 地点 | 状语 | This is the place **where** I was born. |
| **why** | 原因 | 状语 | I know the reason **why** he left. |

### ✍️ 练习 32.1：关系词

填空：The report ____ I sent is attached

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`that/which`

</details>

### 32.2 关系代词 vs 关系副词（判断技巧）

**看关系词在从句中作什么成分：**

| 从句中缺 | 用什么 |
|---|---|
| 缺**主语**或**宾语** | 关系**代词**（who/which/that/whom） |
| 不缺主宾（成分完整） | 关系**副词**（when/where/why）或 "介词 + which" |

**三步判断法：**

1. 先找先行词：它是人、物、时间、地点还是原因？
2. 把先行词暂时放回从句中。
3. 如果句子缺主语或宾语，用关系代词；如果主语和宾语都完整，只需要表示“在那里/在那时/因为这个原因”，用关系副词。

例如：

- This is the book **that** I bought. → I bought **the book**，缺宾语。
- This is the shop **where** I work. → I work **there**，主语和宾语完整。

**注意：** `where` 不是“只要前面有地点名词就能用”。关键是从句是否需要地点状语；如果动词后面缺宾语，应使用 `which/that`。

**经典对比：**

- This is the house **which** he bought.（bought 缺宾语 → 用 which）
- This is the house **where** he lives.（lives 不缺宾语 → 用 where）
- = This is the house **in which** he lives. 这就是他住的房子。

- I'll never forget the day **when** we first met.（met 不缺宾语 → when）
- I'll never forget the day **which** we spent together.（spent 缺宾语 → which）

**关系副词 = 介词 + which：**
| 关系副词 | 等价 |
|---|---|
| when | on/in/at which |
| where | in/at/on which |
| why | for which |

### ✍️ 练习 32.2：关系副词

选择：This is the office (where / which) I work

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`where`

</details>

### 32.3 只能用 that 的情况（超高频考点）

| 情况 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 先行词是 **all, much, little, few, none, everything, anything, nothing, something** | This is **all that** I know. |
| 先行词被 **形容词最高级** 修饰 | This is **the best film that** I've ever seen. |
| 先行词被 **序数词** 修饰 | This is **the first book that** I read. |
| 先行词被 **the only, the very, the same, the last** 修饰 | He is **the only person that** can help. |
| 先行词**既有人又有物** | The **people and things that** we saw... |
| 主句是 **who/which** 开头的疑问句 | **Who** is the man **that** is talking? |
| 先行词是 **人 + 前面有 who** | — |

### ✍️ 练习 32.3：只能用 that

填空：This is the best report ____ I have read

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`that`

</details>

### 32.4 不能用 that 的情况

| 情况 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| **非限制性定语从句**（有逗号） | Tom, **who** is my friend, is a doctor.（不能用 that） |
| **介词后** | The city **in which** I live...（不能用 in that） |

### ✍️ 练习 32.4：不能用 that

选择：Anna, (who / that) works with me, is helpful

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`who`

</details>

### 32.5 限制性 vs 非限制性定语从句（重点）

| 对比 | 限制性 | 非限制性 |
|---|---|---|
| 标点 | **无逗号** | **有逗号** |
| 作用 | 必不可少，去掉句意不完整 | 补充说明，去掉不影响主句 |
| 翻译 | "……的" | 另起一句"他/它……" |
| 用 that | ✅ 可以 | ❌ 不可以 |
| 关系词省略 | 作宾语时可省 | 不可省 |
| 例句 | The man **who** lives next door is a doctor. | Tom, **who** lives next door, is a doctor. |

**意思差别（经典例子）：**
- He has a sister **who** is a doctor.（他有个当医生的姐姐，可能还有别的姐姐）
- He has a sister, **who** is a doctor.（他只有一个姐姐，她是医生）

**⚠️ which 可以指代整个主句**（只在非限制性从句中）：
- He passed the exam, **which** made his parents happy.（which 指"他通过考试"这件事）
- ❌ 不能用 that

### ✍️ 练习 32.5：限制性与非限制性

判断：非限制性定语从句前通常使用逗号。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 32.6 介词 + 关系代词

**结构：** 介词 + which/whom（**不能用 that 或 who**）

- The house **in which** I live is old. 我住的房子很旧。 = The house **which** I live **in** is old.
- The man **to whom** I spoke is my boss. 我和他说话的那个人是我的老板。 = The man **whom** I spoke **to** is my boss.

**⚠️ 介词的选择看动词搭配或先行词：**
- live **in** a house → in which
- depend **on** sb. → on whom
- the reason **for** which = why

**特殊结构：数量词 + of + which/whom**
- I have many books, **most of which** are novels. 我有很多书，其中大部分是小说。
- There are 50 students, **ten of whom** are girls. 有50个学生，其中10个是女生。
- He has three sons, **none of whom** is a doctor. 他有三个儿子，没有一个是医生。

**⚠️ 不能用 them！**
- ❌ I have many books, most of them are novels.（这是两个句子，中间要用 and 或改成 which）

### ✍️ 练习 32.6：介词 + 关系代词

改写：The person to whom I wrote replied

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The person who I wrote to replied.`

</details>

### 32.7 as 引导的定语从句

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| such...as | Such books **as** you need are here. 你需要的这类书都在这里。 |
| the same...as | This is the same book **as** I lost.（同类的） |
| the same...that | This is the same book **that** I lost.（同一本） |
| as...as | as many books **as** you want |
| as 引导非限制性从句（可放句首） | **As** is known to all, the earth is round. |

**⚠️ as 和 which 的区别（非限制性从句）：**
- `as` 表示"正如"，可放**句首、句中、句末**，含**褒义**或"正如所说"
- `which` 只能放**句末**，表示前面整句话的结果

- **As** we all know, he is a good student. 正如我们都知道的那样，他是个好学生。
- He is a good student, **as** we all know. 他是个好学生，这是我们都知道的。
- He failed the exam, **which** made him sad.（which 不能换成 as）

### ✍️ 练习 32.7：as 引导定语从句

填空：As ____ expected, the meeting ended early

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`was`

</details>

### 32.8 关系词的省略

**作宾语的关系代词可以省略（限制性从句中）：**
- The book (**which/that**) I bought is nice. 我买的那本书很不错。
- The man (**whom/who/that**) I met is Tom. 我遇见的那个人是汤姆。

**⚠️ 作主语的不能省：**
- ❌ The man is Tom called me. → ✅ The man **who** called me is Tom. 给我打电话的那个人是汤姆。

**⚠️ 介词提前时不能省：**
- The house **in which** I live...（in which 不能省）

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **which / where 混用**：看从句成分是否完整
2. **非限制性从句用 that**
3. **重复主语/宾语**：
   - ❌ The man who he called me is Tom.（who 已经是主语了）
   - ❌ The book which I bought it is nice.（which 已经是宾语了）
4. **主谓一致看先行词**：
   - ✅ He is one of the students **who are** good at English.（先行词是 students）
   - ✅ He is **the only one** of the students **who is** good at English.（the only one → 单数）

### ✍️ 练习 32.8：关系词省略

判断：The book (that) I bought is useful. 中 that 可以省略。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### ✍️ 追加练习 32：定语从句综合

选择关系词或合并句子：

1. This is the book ____ I told you about

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`that/which`

</details>
2. This is the office ____ I work

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`where`

</details>
3. The woman ____ son is a doctor lives next door

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`whose`

</details>
4. 合并：`I met a man. He spoke five languages.`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I met a man who spoke five languages.`

</details>
5. 改错：`This is the place which I was born.`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`This is the place where I was born.`

</details>

填入合适的关系词：

## 33. 名词性从句

### 讲解

**名词性从句 = 一个句子充当名词的角色。** 分四种：**主语从句、宾语从句、表语从句、同位语从句**。

### 33.1 连接词总览

| 类别 | 连接词 | 是否有含义 | 从句中作成分 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **连词** | that | 无义 | 不作成分 |
| | whether / if | 是否 | 不作成分 |
| **连接代词** | what, who, whom, whose, which, whatever, whoever, whichever | 有义 | 主语/宾语/定语 |
| **连接副词** | when, where, why, how, however, whenever, wherever | 有义 | 状语 |

**判断技巧：**
1. 从句**成分完整** + 无需翻译 → 用 **that**
2. 从句**成分完整** + 表"是否" → 用 **whether/if**
3. 从句**缺主语/宾语** → 用 **what / who / which**
4. 从句**缺状语** → 用 **when / where / why / how**

### ✍️ 练习 33.1：连接词

选择：I know (that / what) he is busy

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`that`

</details>

### 33.2 宾语从句（最常用）

**结构：** 主句 + 连接词 + **陈述语序**从句

**三大要素（考试必考）：**

#### ① 语序：必须用陈述语序！

- ❌ I don't know where **is he**. 我不知道他在哪儿。
- ✅ I don't know where **he is**. 我不知道他在哪儿。
- ❌ Can you tell me what **is this**? 你能告诉我这是什么吗？
- ✅ Can you tell me what **this is**? 你能告诉我这是什么吗？

#### ② 时态：主句过去时，从句用相应的过去时态

| 主句 | 从句 |
|---|---|
| 现在时 | 任何时态都可以 |
| **过去时** | 必须用**过去范畴**的时态（过去时、过去进行、过去完成、过去将来） |

- He **said** that he **was** busy.（不是 is）
- He **said** that he **had finished** it.（不是 has finished）
- He **said** that he **would** come.（不是 will）

**⚠️ 例外：从句是客观真理，永远用一般现在时**
- He said that the earth **is** round.（不是 was）

#### ③ 连接词的选择

| 情况 | 连接词 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 陈述句变宾语从句 | that（口语可省） | He said (that) he was busy. |
| 一般疑问句变宾语从句 | **whether / if** | I don't know **whether/if** he will come. |
| 特殊疑问句变宾语从句 | 保留原疑问词 | I don't know **when** he will come. |

**⚠️ 只能用 whether 不能用 if 的情况：**

| 情况 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 介词后 | It depends on **whether** he comes. |
| 与 or not 直接连用 | I don't know **whether or not** he'll come. |
| 后接不定式 | I don't know **whether to go**. |
| 引导主语从句（句首） | **Whether** he comes is not important. |
| 引导表语从句 | The question is **whether** he will come. |
| 引导同位语从句 | — |

### ✍️ 练习 33.2：宾语从句语序

改正：I know where is he

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I know where he is.`

</details>

### 33.3 主语从句

**结构一：从句直接作主语**
- **What he said** is true. 他说的是真的。
- **That he will come** is certain. 他会来是肯定的。
- **Whether he comes** doesn't matter. 他来不来无所谓。

**结构二：用 it 作形式主语（更常用）**
- **It** is true **that he said so**. 他这么说是真的。
- **It** doesn't matter **whether he comes**. 他来不来无所谓。

**常见形式主语句型：**

| 句型 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| It is + 形容词 + that... | **It is important that** we study hard. |
| It is + 名词 + that... | **It is a pity that** he failed. |
| It is said/reported/believed + that... | **It is said that** he is rich. 据说他很富有。 |
| It seems/happens + that... | **It seems that** he is right. 看来他是对的。 |
| It doesn't matter + wh-... | **It doesn't matter what** you do. 你做什么都没关系。 |

**⚠️ 主语从句谓语用单数：**
- ✅ **What he said is** true. 他说的是真的。
- ✅ **When and where we will meet has** not been decided. 我们什么时候、在哪里见面还没决定。

### ✍️ 练习 33.3：主语从句

翻译：他是否参加很重要

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Whether he will attend is important.`

</details>

### 33.4 表语从句

**结构：** 主语 + be/系动词 + 连接词 + 从句

- The problem is **that we have no money**. 问题是我们没有钱。
- The question is **whether he will come**. 问题是他会不会来。
- This is **what I want**. 这就是我想要的。
- That's **why he was late**. 这就是他迟到的原因。
- It looks **as if it is going to rain**. 看起来好像要下雨了。

**⚠️ 特殊：** `The reason is that...`（不能用 because）
- ❌ The reason is because he was ill. 原因是他病了。
- ✅ The reason is **that** he was ill. 原因是他病了。

### ✍️ 练习 33.4：表语从句

填空：The problem is ____ we are late

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`that`

</details>

### 33.5 同位语从句

**作用：解释说明前面的抽象名词。**

**常见抽象名词：** `fact, news, idea, hope, belief, thought, question, problem, doubt, suggestion, order, possibility, conclusion, message, evidence, opinion`

- The news **that he won the game** made us happy.（新闻内容 = 他赢了）
- I have no idea **what he means**. 我不知道他是什么意思。
- The question **whether he is guilty** is unclear. 他是否有罪这个问题还不清楚。

**⚠️ 同位语从句 vs 定语从句（超高频难点）：**

| 对比 | 同位语从句 | 定语从句 |
|---|---|---|
| that 的作用 | **连词**，不作成分，不能省 | **关系代词**，作主语/宾语 |
| 从句结构 | **完整** | **不完整**（缺主语或宾语） |
| 关系 | 从句 **=** 先行词内容 | 从句**修饰**先行词 |
| 例句 | The news **that he won** is true.（新闻=他赢了） | The news **that he told me** is true.（told 缺宾语） |

**判断技巧：** 把 that 后面的句子单独拿出来，成分完整 → 同位语从句；缺成分 → 定语从句。

### ✍️ 练习 33.5：同位语从句

判断：the fact that he left 中 that 引导同位语从句。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 33.6 that 不能省略的情况

1. 引导**主语从句**（除 it 形式主语外）
2. 引导**表语从句**
3. 引导**同位语从句**
4. 宾语从句中，**并列的第二个 that** 不能省
   - He said (that) he was busy and **that** he couldn't come. 他说他很忙，而且不能来。
5. **介词后**的宾语从句

### ✍️ 练习 33.6：that 不能省略

填空：The fact ____ he won surprised us

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`that`

</details>

### 33.7 what 和 that 的区别（高频）

| 词 | 从句中作成分 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **that** | **不作成分** | 无义 | I know **that** he is right.（he is right 完整） |
| **what** | 作**主语/宾语/表语** | "……的东西/事情" | I know **what** he wants.（wants 缺宾语） |

- ✅ **What** he said is true.（said 缺宾语 → what）
- ✅ **That** he said so is true.（said so 完整 → that）

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **宾语从句用疑问语序**（最高频）
2. **主句过去时，从句时态没变**
3. **The reason is because**（应该用 that）
4. **what / that 混用**
5. **whether / if 用错**（介词后只能用 whether）

### ✍️ 练习 33.7：what 与 that

选择：I understand (what / that) you mean

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`what`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 33

填入合适的连接词：

1. I don't know ___ he will come or not. 我不知道他会不会来。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

</details>
2. ___ he said is not true. 他说的不是真的。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：What

</details>
3. The question is ___ we can finish it on time. 问题是我们能不能按时完成。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：whether

</details>
4. I have no idea ___ he lives. 我不知道他住在哪里。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：where

</details>
5. The news ___ he won the match excited us. 他赢得比赛的消息让我们很兴奋。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：that

</details>
6. It depends on ___ he agrees. 这取决于他是否同意。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：whether

</details>
7. That's ___ I want to say. 这就是我想说的。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：what

</details>
8. The reason is ___ he was ill. 原因是他病了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：that

改错：

9. ❌ Do you know where does he live? 你知道他住在哪里吗？

答案：✅ Do you know where **he lives**?

<summary>查看答案</summary>

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ The reason is **that** he was late.

</details>
12. ❌ I don't know if or not he will come. 我不知道他会不会来。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ I don't know **whether** or not he will come.

</details>

## 34. 状语从句

### 讲解

状语从句修饰**主句的动词、形容词或整个句子**，说明时间、地点、原因、条件等。共 **9 大类**。

### 34.1 时间状语从句

| 连词 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| when | 当……时 | **When** I got home, he was cooking. |
| while | 当……时（持续） | **While** I was reading, he came. |
| as | 一边……一边；随着 | **As** he grew older, he became wiser. |
| before | 在……之前 | Finish it **before** you leave. |
| after | 在……之后 | **After** he left, I called you. |
| until / till | 直到 | Wait **until** I come back. |
| since | 自从 | I have been here **since** I was 10. |
| as soon as | 一……就 | **As soon as** he came, we left. |
| the moment / the minute | 一……就 | **The moment** I saw her, I knew. |
| by the time | 到……时候 | **By the time** you arrive, I'll be gone. |
| every time / each time | 每次 | **Every time** I see him, he's busy. |
| whenever | 无论何时 | Come **whenever** you like. |
| hardly...when / no sooner...than | 一……就 | **Hardly** had I sat down **when** it rang. |

**⚠️ not...until 句型（重点）：**
- He **didn't** go to bed **until** his mother came back. 直到妈妈回来他才睡觉。
- 强调句型：**It was not until** his mother came back **that** he went to bed.
- 倒装：**Not until** his mother came back **did he go** to bed.

**⚠️ 主将从现：** 时间状语从句用一般现在时表将来。

### ✍️ 练习 34.1：时间状语从句

填空：Call me when you ____ (arrive)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`arrive`

</details>

### 34.2 地点状语从句

**连词：** where, wherever, anywhere, everywhere

- Put it **where** you found it. 把它放回你找到它的地方。
- **Wherever** you go, I'll follow you. 无论你去哪里，我都会跟着你。

**⚠️ 地点状语从句 vs 定语从句：**
- This is the place **where** we met.（定语从句，修饰 place）
- Put it **where** you found it. 把它放回你找到它的地方。（状语从句，前面没有先行词）

### 34.3 原因状语从句

| 连词 | 语气 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| because | 最强，回答 why | I stayed home **because** it rained. |
| since | 既然（已知原因） | **Since** you're busy, I'll go alone. |
| as | 由于（次要原因） | **As** it was late, we went home. |
| now that | 既然 | **Now that** you're here, let's start. |
| for | 补充理由（并列连词） | He must be ill, **for** he looks pale. |
| seeing that / considering that | 鉴于 | **Seeing that** he's a child, forgive him. |

**⚠️ because 不能和 so 连用！**

### 34.4 条件状语从句

| 连词 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| if | 如果 | **If** it rains, I won't go. |
| unless | 除非（= if not） | I won't go **unless** you come. |
| as long as / so long as | 只要 | **As long as** you try, you'll succeed. |
| in case | 以防 | Take a coat **in case** it gets cold. |
| provided/providing that | 假如 | **Provided that** you agree, we'll start. |
| suppose/supposing that | 假如 | **Supposing** it rains, what shall we do? |
| on condition that | 条件是 | I'll go **on condition that** you come too. |
| once | 一旦 | **Once** you start, you can't stop. |

**⚠️ 主将从现同样适用。**

### 34.5 让步状语从句

| 连词 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| though / although | 虽然 | **Although** he is poor, he is happy. |
| even if / even though | 即使 | **Even if** it rains, I'll go. |
| while | 尽管（句首） | **While** I admit it's hard, I'll try. |
| as | 尽管（**必须倒装**） | Young **as** he is, he knows a lot. |
| whether...or... | 无论 | **Whether** you like it **or** not, you must do it. |
| no matter + wh- | 无论 | **No matter what** you say, I won't go. |
| wh-ever | 无论 | **Whatever** you say, I won't go. |

**⚠️ as 引导让步从句必须倒装（把表语/状语/动词提前）：**
- **Child as** he is, he knows a lot.（名词提前**不加冠词**）
- **Hard as** he works, he can't pass. 尽管他很努力，他还是通不过。
- **Try as** he might, he failed. 尽管他尽力了，他还是失败了。

**⚠️ no matter what vs whatever：**
- `no matter what` 只引导**状语从句**
- `whatever` 可引导**状语从句**和**名词性从句**
- ✅ **Whatever** you say is right.（主语从句，不能用 no matter what）
- ✅ **No matter what/Whatever** you say, I won't go.（状语从句，都行）

### 34.6 目的状语从句

| 连词 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| so that | He got up early **so that** he could catch the train. 他早起是为了赶上火车。 |
| in order that | He works hard **in order that** he can pass. 他努力学习是为了能通过考试。 |
| lest / for fear that | Take care **lest** you (should) fall. 小心，以免摔倒。 |

**⚠️ 目的状语从句常带**情态动词**（can, could, may, might, should）。

### 34.7 结果状语从句

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| so that | It was raining, **so that** we stayed home. 当时在下雨，所以我们待在家里。 |
| so + adj./adv. + that | He is **so** tired **that** he can't work. 他太累了，以至于无法工作。 |
| such + n. + that | He is **such** a nice man **that** we all like him. 他是个很好的人，我们都喜欢他。 |
| so + adj. + a/an + n. + that | He is **so** nice **a** man **that**... 他是如此友善的一个人，以至于…… |
| so + many/much/few/little + n. + that | There were **so many** people **that** I couldn't move. 人太多了，我都动不了。 |

**⚠️ so that 既可表目的也可表结果，区别：**
- 目的：从句常有 can/could/may/might，**无逗号**
- 结果：从句是事实陈述，**常有逗号**

### 34.8 比较状语从句

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| than | He is taller **than** I (am). 他比我高。 |
| as...as | He is **as** tall **as** I (am). 他和我一样高。 |
| not so/as...as | He is **not as** tall **as** I. 他没有我高。 |
| the more...the more | **The more** you learn, **the more** you know. 你学得越多，知道得就越多。 |

### 34.9 方式状语从句

| 连词 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| as | Do **as** I say. 按我说的做。 |
| as if / as though | He talks **as if** he knew everything. 他说话就好像什么都知道似的。 |
| the way | Do it **the way** I showed you. 按我教你的方法做。 |

**⚠️ as if 后的虚拟语气：**
- 与**现在**事实相反 → 从句用**过去时**：He talks as if he **knew** everything.
- 与**过去**事实相反 → 从句用**过去完成时**：He talks as if he **had been** there.
- **可能是真的** → 用**陈述语气**：It looks as if it **is going to** rain.

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **although + but 连用**
2. **because + so 连用**
3. **主将从现忘记**
4. **as 引导让步从句不倒装**
5. **no matter what 引导名词性从句**（应用 whatever）
6. **so / such 用错**
