## 35. 非谓语动词详解

### 讲解

**非谓语动词 = 不作谓语的动词形式。** 三大类：**不定式（to do）、动名词（doing）、分词（doing / done）**。

### 35.1 三大非谓语总览

| 类型 | 形式 | 性质 | 主要功能 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 不定式 | to do | 名词/形容词/副词 | 主语、宾语、表语、定语、状语、宾补 |
| 动名词 | doing | 名词 | 主语、宾语、表语、定语 |
| 现在分词 | doing | 形容词/副词 | 定语、表语、状语、宾补 |
| 过去分词 | done | 形容词/副词 | 定语、表语、状语、宾补 |

**非谓语选择的核心不是“看起来像什么”，而是看它和逻辑主语的关系：**

- 主语主动做动作，常用 `doing`：`The girl standing by the door is my sister.`
- 主语被动作影响，常用 `done`：`The letter written by Tom is on the desk.`
- 表示目的、计划或将来的动作，常用 `to do`：`I have a letter to write.`

**对比：**

- `the man **repairing** the car`：这个人正在修车。
- `the car **repaired** yesterday`：这辆车昨天被修好了。
- `the tools **to repair** the car`：用来修车的工具。

**一句话小结：** `doing` 看主动或进行，`done` 看被动或完成，`to do` 常看目的、计划或尚未完成的动作；还要检查非谓语的逻辑主语是否正确。

### ✍️ 练习 35.1：非谓语总览

判断：To learn、learning、learned 都可能作非谓语形式。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 35.2 分词的核心区别：doing vs done（超高频）

**判断标准：逻辑主语与分词是"主动"还是"被动"关系**

| 关系 | 用哪个 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **主动 / 进行** | **doing**（现在分词） | the **rising** sun 升起的太阳 |
| **被动 / 完成** | **done**（过去分词） | the **fallen** leaves 落下的叶子 |

**经典对比：**

| 现在分词 | 过去分词 |
|---|---|
| a **surprising** result（令人惊讶的结果）| a **surprised** look（惊讶的表情）|
| the **exciting** news | the **excited** crowd |
| **boiling** water（沸腾的水）| **boiled** water（开水，已烧开）|
| a **developing** country（发展中国家）| a **developed** country（发达国家）|
| the **leading** country（领先的国家）| the **lost** child（走失的孩子）|

### ✍️ 练习 35.2：doing 与 done

选择：The email (sending / sent) yesterday needs a reply

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`sent`

</details>

### 35.3 分词作状语（重点难点）

**规则：分词的逻辑主语必须是主句的主语！**

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 时间 | **Walking** in the street, I met an old friend. |
| 原因 | **Being** ill, he didn't go to school. |
| 条件 | **Working** hard, you will succeed. |
| 让步 | **Though defeated**, he didn't lose heart. |
| 伴随 | He sat there, **reading** a book. |
| 结果 | He fell down, **breaking** his leg. |
| 方式 | He left, **saying** nothing. |

**主动 vs 被动：**
- **Seeing** the teacher, the students stood up.（学生看见 → 主动）
- **Seen** from the hill, the city looks beautiful.（城市被看 → 被动）

**完成式（强调先后）：**
- **Having finished** his work, he went home.（先完成工作，再回家）
- **Having been told** many times, he still made the mistake. 尽管被告知了很多次，他还是犯了这个错误。

**否定式：** `not + 分词`
- **Not knowing** what to do, he asked for help. 因为不知道该做什么，他寻求了帮助。

### ✍️ 练习 35.3：分词作状语

改写：When I was walking home, I saw the rain start.

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：Walking home, I saw the rain start. 句中 Walking 作状语。

</details>

### 35.4 垂悬分词（经典错误）

**分词的逻辑主语必须和主句主语一致，否则就错了。**

- ❌ **Walking** in the street, a car hit me.（走路的是"车"？）
- ✅ **Walking** in the street, **I** was hit by a car. 我走在街上时被一辆车撞了。
- ✅ **While I was walking** in the street, a car hit me. 我走在街上时被一辆车撞了。

- ❌ **Seen** from the hill, we found the city beautiful. 从山上看，这座城市很美。
- ✅ **Seen** from the hill, **the city** looks beautiful. 从山上看，这座城市很美。

### ✍️ 练习 35.4：垂悬分词

改正：Walking down the street, the car hit me

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Walking down the street, I was hit by a car.`

</details>

### 35.5 非谓语作定语的区别

| 形式 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **doing** | 正在进行、主动 | the **sleeping** boy（正在睡的男孩） |
| **done** | 已完成、被动 | the **broken** window（被打破的窗户） |
| **to do** | 将要、未发生 | the meeting **to be held**（将举行的会议） |

**对比：**
- the problem **being discussed**（正在讨论的问题）
- the problem **discussed** yesterday（昨天讨论过的问题）
- the problem **to be discussed** tomorrow（明天要讨论的问题）

### ✍️ 练习 35.5：非谓语作定语

选择：the people (waiting / waited) outside

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`waiting`

</details>

### 35.6 非谓语作宾语补足语

| 结构 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **see/hear/watch sb. do** | 看见做了**全过程** | I saw him **cross** the road. |
| **see/hear/watch sb. doing** | 看见**正在**做 | I saw him **crossing** the road. |
| **see/hear sth. done** | 看见……**被** | I saw the door **opened**. |
| **have sth. done** | ①让别人做 ②遭遇 | I had my hair **cut**. / He had his leg **broken**. |
| **have sb. do** | 让某人做 | I had him **repair** the car. |
| **have sb./sth. doing** | 让……一直做 | Don't have the water **running**. |
| **make/let sb. do** | 使/让某人做 | He made me **laugh**. |
| **get sth. done** | 使某事被做 | I got my car **repaired**. |
| **get sb. to do** | 说服某人做 | I got him **to help** me. |
| **keep sb./sth. doing** | 使一直做 | Sorry to keep you **waiting**. |
| **find sb. doing** | 发现正在做 | I found him **sleeping**. |
| **want/wish sb. to do** | 想让某人做 | I want you **to go**. |

**⚠️ have sth. done 的三种含义：**
1. 让别人做：I **had my car washed**.（我让人洗了车）
2. 遭遇不幸：He **had his wallet stolen**.（他钱包被偷了）
3. 完成某事：I **have my homework done**.（作业做完了）

### ✍️ 练习 35.6：非谓语作宾补

判断：I saw him running. 中 running 作宾语补足语。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 35.7 独立主格与 with 复合结构（预告，详见第 40 章）

- **The meeting being over**, we all went home.（独立主格）
- He sat there **with his eyes closed**.（with 复合结构）

### ✍️ 练习 35.7：独立主格预览

翻译：天气允许的话，我们会出发

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Weather permitting, we will leave.`

</details>

### 35.8 非谓语选择流程图

```
1️⃣ 句子已有谓语吗？
   没有 → 用谓语动词
   有 → 用非谓语，继续 ↓

2️⃣ 作什么成分？
   主语/宾语/表语 → doing 或 to do
   定语/状语/宾补 → 继续 ↓

3️⃣ 逻辑主语与动作是主动还是被动？
   主动 → doing / to do
   被动 → done / to be done

4️⃣ 动作发生的时间？
   正在进行 → doing
   已经完成 → done / having done
   将要发生 → to do / to be done
```

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **垂悬分词**（逻辑主语不一致）
2. **doing / done 主被动搞反**
3. **一个句子两个谓语**：
   - ❌ He entered the room, sat down. 他走进房间，坐了下来。
   - ✅ He entered the room **and sat down**. 他走进房间，坐了下来。 / He entered the room, **sitting down**.
4. **感官动词被动忘记加 to**
5. **-ing / -ed 形容词用反**

答案：Seen

3. ___ (not know) what to do, he asked for help. 因为不知道该做什么，他寻求了帮助。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：Not knowing

</details>
4. ___ (finish) his homework, he went out to play.（用完成式）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：Having finished

</details>
5. The problem ___ (discuss) tomorrow is important. 明天要讨论的这个问题很重要。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：to be discussed

</details>
6. The window ___ (break) yesterday has been repaired. 昨天被打破的窗户已经修好了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：broken

</details>
7. I saw him ___ (cross) the road and reach the other side. 我看见他过了马路，到了对面。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：cross

</details>
8. I had my hair ___ (cut) yesterday. 我昨天理了发。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：cut

</details>
9. He sat there with his eyes ___ (close). 他坐在那里，闭着眼睛。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：closed

</details>
10. Don't keep me ___ (wait) too long. 别让我等太久。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：waiting

</details>

改错：

11. ❌ Walking in the street, a car hit me. 我走在街上时，一辆车撞了我。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ Walking in the street, **I was hit by a car**.

</details>

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ Seen from the hill, **the city is beautiful**.

</details>

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ He was made **to work** overtime.

14. ❌ The film is very interested. 这部电影很有趣。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ The film is very **interesting**.

## 🌐 第三阶段综合任务：解释一个工作流程

> **任务目标：** 使用完成时、被动语态和从句说明一件事情如何完成，以及为什么这样做。

### 1. 听力：客户订单处理流程

请让学习伙伴朗读下文，或用文本转语音工具播放两遍。记录已经完成的步骤、正在等待的步骤和负责各步骤的人。

> The customer placed the order on Monday. Since then, the payment has been confirmed and the products have been packed. The delivery company, which collects orders every afternoon, has not arrived yet. As soon as the driver arrives, the package will be sent to the customer.
>
> 客户星期一下了订单。从那以后，付款已经确认，商品也已经打包。每天下午取件的快递公司还没有到。司机一到，包裹就会寄给客户。

听后回答：Which steps have been completed?（哪些步骤已经完成？）What has not happened yet?（什么事情还没有发生？）When will the package be sent?（包裹什么时候寄出？）

### 2. 阅读：拆解复杂句

找出文中的现在完成时、两个被动结构、定语从句和时间状语从句。把最后一句改写成两个简单句，再比较哪一种表达更紧凑。

### 3. 口语：说明熟悉的流程

选择“提交请假申请”“网上购物”或“准备一次会议”，连续讲述2分钟。至少使用：

- 两个顺序连接词，如 `first`、`then`、`after that`
- 一个完成时
- 两个被动结构
- 一个定语从句或状语从句

### 4. 写作：编写操作说明

写一篇120至150词的流程说明。开头说明流程目的，中间按顺序解释步骤，结尾说明完成标准。不要为了使用高级结构而写过长的句子。

**参考开头：** `This process is used to...` / `Before the request is approved, ...` / `The person who reviews the request...`

**达标标准：** 能听出完成与未完成的步骤；能够准确拆分复杂句；口语持续2分钟且顺序清楚；写作中的从句逻辑和主被动关系正确率达到80%。

---
---

# 第四阶段 · 高级语法

> **本阶段目标：** 能读懂正式英语中常见的强调、倒装、省略、虚拟和复杂一致关系。
>
> **学习提醒：** 高级结构不等于日常交流的必用结构。先理解它表达的语气和逻辑，再记句型。

---

## 36. 虚拟语气

### 讲解

> **先理解：** 虚拟语气表达的不是普通事实，而是假设、愿望、遗憾、建议，或与现实相反的情况。

**虚拟语气 = 说的不是事实，而是假设、愿望、建议。**

**先区分事实和假设：**

- 事实：**If it rains**, we **will stay** home.（下雨是可能发生的。）
- 与现在事实相反：**If it rained**, we **would stay** home.（说话人认为现在下雨的可能性很小，或事实上没有下雨。）
- 与过去事实相反：**If it had rained**, we **would have stayed** home.（事实上过去没有下雨，也没有待在家里。）

所以，虚拟语气中的“过去式”不一定表示过去时间，它有时表示“距离现实更远”。判断重点是：条件是真实可能，还是与事实相反。

英语有三种语气：
- **陈述语气**：说事实（He is here.）
- **祈使语气**：命令（Be here!）
- **虚拟语气**：非事实（If he **were** here...）

### 36.1 if 虚拟条件句（核心三种，必背表）

| 情况 | if 从句 | 主句 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **与现在相反** | 过去式（be 用 **were**） | would/could/might/should + **do** | **If** I **were** you, I **would go**. |
| **与过去相反** | **had done** | would/could/might/should + **have done** | **If** I **had known**, I **would have told** you. |
| **与将来相反** | ① 过去式<br>② **were to do**<br>③ **should do** | would/could/might/should + **do** | **If** it **should rain** tomorrow, we **would** cancel it. |

**记忆口诀：**
```
现在虚拟：从句过去式，主句 would do
过去虚拟：从句 had done，主句 would have done
将来虚拟：从句 should do / were to do，主句 would do
（主句时态永远比真实情况"退一步"）
```

**使用顺序：** 先判断时间，再判断真假：

1. 现在或将来，且条件真实可能：用普通条件句。
2. 现在不真实：`if + 过去式`，主句 `would + do`。
3. 过去没有发生：`if + had done`，主句 `would have done`。

不要看到 `if` 就自动使用虚拟语气；普通条件句和虚拟条件句的区别，首先取决于说话人对条件是否当真的判断。

**例句详解：**

**与现在相反：**
- **If I were** a bird, I **would fly** to you.（我不是鸟）
- **If he had** money, he **would buy** a car.（他没钱）

**与过去相反：**
- **If you had come** earlier, you **would have seen** him.（你没早来）
- **If I had studied** hard, I **would have passed**.（我没努力学）

**与将来相反（可能性极小）：**
- **If it should rain** tomorrow, we **would stay** home. 如果明天万一下雨，我们就待在家里。
- **If he were to come**, I **would be** happy. 如果他来的话，我会很高兴。

### ✍️ 练习 36.1：if 虚拟条件句

填空：If I ____ (be) you, I would accept

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`were`

</details>

### 36.2 错综时间条件句（混合虚拟，高频考点）

**从句和主句的时间不一致时，各按各的时间来。**

- **If you had followed** my advice（过去）, you **would be** healthy now（现在）.
  如果你当初听我的，你现在就健康了。
- **If he were** careful（现在的性格）, he **wouldn't have made** that mistake（过去）.
  如果他细心的话，他就不会犯那个错了。

### ✍️ 练习 36.2：混合虚拟

判断：混合虚拟需要根据时间分别选择时态。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 36.3 虚拟条件句的倒装（省略 if）

**当从句中有 were / had / should 时，可省略 if，把这三个词提前，构成倒装。**

| 原句 | 倒装 |
|---|---|
| If I **were** you... 如果我是你…… | **Were I** you... |
| If I **had known**... 如果我早知道…… | **Had I known**... |
| If it **should rain**... 如果下雨…… | **Should it rain**... |

- **Were I** you, I would go. 如果我是你，我就会去。
- **Had I known** it, I would have told you. 如果我早知道，我就会告诉你了。
- **Should he come**, tell him to wait. 万一他来了，告诉他等一下。

**⚠️ 倒装后的否定不能缩写：**
- ✅ **Had it not been** for your help... 要不是有你的帮助……
- ❌ Hadn't it been for... 要不是……

### ✍️ 练习 36.3：虚拟倒装

改写：If I were you..

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Were I you...`

</details>

### 36.4 含蓄条件句（没有 if，但有虚拟含义）

| 表达 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **but for** | 要不是 | **But for** your help, I would have failed. |
| **without** | 没有 | **Without** water, we couldn't live. |
| **otherwise** | 否则 | I was busy; **otherwise** I would have come. |
| **or** | 否则 | Hurry up, **or** you will be late. |
| 介词短语 | — | **In** your **position**, I would accept it. |

### ✍️ 练习 36.4：含蓄条件

判断：Without your help, I would fail. 含有虚拟条件。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 36.5 wish 后的虚拟语气（超高频）

**wish 后的宾语从句永远用虚拟语气：**

| 时间 | 从句形式 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 与**现在**相反 | 过去式（be 用 were） | I **wish** I **were** rich. 但愿我有钱。 |
| 与**过去**相反 | **had done** | I **wish** I **had studied** harder. 但愿我当初努力了。 |
| 与**将来**相反 | **would / could do** | I **wish** it **would stop** raining. 但愿雨能停。 |

**⚠️ if only 用法完全相同，但语气更强烈：**
- **If only** I **were** young again! 要是我能再年轻一次就好了！

### ✍️ 练习 36.5：wish 虚拟

填空：I wish I ____ (have) more time

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`had`

</details>

### 36.6 "一坚持二命令三建议四要求"（should + 动词原形）

**以下动词后的宾语从句用 `(should) + 动词原形`，should 可省略：**

| 类别 | 动词 |
|---|---|
| 坚持 | insist |
| 命令 | order, command |
| 建议 | suggest, advise, propose, recommend |
| 要求 | demand, request, require, desire, ask, urge |

- He **suggested** that we **(should) go** at once. 他建议我们立刻走。
- The doctor **ordered** that he **(should) stay** in bed. 医生要求他卧床休息。
- I **insist** that he **(should) be** punished. 我坚持要求他受到惩罚。

**⚠️ 陷阱：insist 和 suggest 表示"坚持认为"和"暗示"时，用陈述语气！**
- He **insisted** that he **was** right.（坚持认为，事实）
- His silence **suggested** that he **agreed**.（暗示，事实）
- He **insisted** that we **(should) leave**.（坚持要求，虚拟）

**⚠️ 对应的名词/形容词句型也用虚拟：**
- His **suggestion** is that we **(should) start** early.（表语从句）
- The **order** that we **(should) leave** was clear.（同位语从句）
- It is **necessary/important/strange/natural/a pity** that... + **(should) do**
  - It is necessary that he **(should) come**. 他有必要来。

**常见形容词：** necessary, important, essential, vital, urgent, strange, natural, surprising, a pity, no wonder

### ✍️ 练习 36.6：建议类虚拟

填空：They suggested that he ____ (go) earlier

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`go`

</details>

### 36.7 其他虚拟语气句型

| 句型 | 规则 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **would rather + 从句** | 现在/将来用过去式，过去用过去完成式 | I **would rather** you **came** tomorrow. |
| **as if / as though** | 与现在相反用过去式，与过去相反用过去完成式 | He talks **as if** he **knew** everything. |
| **It's (high/about) time that** | + 过去式 或 should do | It's **high time** that we **went** home. |
| **lest / for fear that / in case** | + (should) do | Be careful **lest** you **(should) fall**. 要小心，以免摔倒。 |
| **may + 动词原形**（祝愿） | 倒装 | **May** you **succeed**! |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **be 动词在虚拟中用 were**（所有人称）：
   - ✅ If I **were** you...（不是 was）
2. **主从句形式搞混**：
   - ❌ If I would be you... → ✅ If I **were** you... 如果我是你……
3. **wish 后用现在时**：
   - ❌ I wish I am rich. → ✅ I wish I **were** rich. 我希望我很富有。
4. **suggest 表"建议"和"暗示"的区别**
5. **倒装后缩写否定**

### ✍️ 练习 36.7：其他虚拟句型

翻译：但愿明天不下雨

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`If only it would not rain tomorrow.`

</details>

## 37. 倒装句

### 讲解

**倒装 = 把谓语（或助动词）放到主语前面。**

分两种：
- **完全倒装**：整个谓语放主语前
- **部分倒装**：只把助动词/情态动词/be 动词放主语前

### 37.1 完全倒装（谓语全部提前）

| 情况 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| **there be** 句型 | **There is** a book on the desk. |
| **地点副词/介词短语**放句首（主语是名词） | **On the wall hangs** a picture. |
| | **Here comes** the bus! 公交车来了！ |
| | **There goes** the bell! 铃响了！ |
| | **Out rushed** the children. 孩子们冲了出去。 |
| **表语**放句首 | **Present at the meeting were** many students. |
| | **Gone are** the days when... 那些日子已经一去不复返了…… |
| **so/neither/nor** 表示"也（不）" | — |

**⚠️ 主语是代词时不倒装：**
- ✅ **Here he comes**!（不是 Here comes he）
- ✅ **Here comes the bus**!（主语是名词，倒装）

### ✍️ 练习 37.1：完全倒装

改写：A bus comes here

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Here comes a bus.`

</details>

### 37.2 部分倒装（只提助动词）

**构成：** 助动词/情态动词/be 动词 + 主语 + 其余部分
如果没有助动词，要**加 do/does/did**。

#### ① 否定词放句首（超高频）

**否定词：** never, seldom, rarely, hardly, scarcely, barely, little, few, no, not, nowhere, by no means, in no way, at no time, under no circumstances, no sooner, not only, not until

- **Never have I** seen such a thing. 我从未见过这样的事。
- **Seldom does he** go there. 他很少去那里。
- **Little did I** know that he was lying. 我一点也不知道他在撒谎。
- **Not until** he came back **did I** leave. 直到他回来我才离开。
- **Hardly had I** arrived **when** it began to rain. 我刚到就开始下雨了。
- **No sooner had he** left **than** it rained. 他刚一离开就下雨了。
- **Not only did he** come, **but he also** helped. 他不但来了，还帮了忙。

**⚠️ 否定词在句中不倒装：**
- ✅ I have **never** seen such a thing.（正常语序）

**⚠️ not until 的倒装只倒主句：**
- **Not until** he came back **did I leave**.（从句 he came back 不倒装）

#### ② only + 状语放句首

- **Only then did I** realize my mistake. 直到那时我才意识到自己的错误。
- **Only in this way can we** succeed. 只有这样我们才能成功。
- **Only when he came back did I** leave.（only + 状语从句，倒装主句）

**⚠️ only 修饰主语时不倒装：**
- ✅ **Only he** knows the answer. 只有他知道答案。

#### ③ so / neither / nor 表示"也（不）"

| 结构 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **So + 助动词 + 主语** | 前面的情况也适用于后者 | He is tall. — **So am I**. |
| **Neither/Nor + 助动词 + 主语** | 否定的"也不" | He can't swim. — **Neither can I**. |
| **主语 + 助动词 + so**（不倒装） | 确认前面所说属实 | It's cold today. — **So it is**.（确实如此） |

**对比记忆：**
- — I like coffee. — **So do I**.（我也喜欢）
- — It's a nice day. — **So it is**.（确实是好天气）

#### ④ 虚拟条件句省略 if（见第 36 章）

- **Were I** you... / **Had I known**... / **Should he come**... 如果我是你……/要是我早知道……/万一他来了……

#### ⑤ as / though 引导让步状语从句

**结构：** 表语/状语/动词原形 + as/though + 主语 + 谓语

- **Young as** he is, he knows a lot.（形容词提前）
- **Child as** he is, he knows a lot.（名词提前，**不加冠词**）
- **Hard as** he works, he can't pass. 尽管他很努力，他还是通不过。（副词提前）
- **Try as** he might, he failed. 尽管他尽力了，他还是失败了。（动词原形提前）

#### ⑥ 感叹句/祝愿句

- **May** you **succeed**!
- **Long live** the People's Republic of China! 中华人民共和国万岁！

#### ⑦ so...that / such...that 中 so/such 放句首

- **So loudly did he** speak **that** everyone heard him. 他说话声音太大了，大家都听到了。
- **Such was** the force of the explosion **that** all the windows broke. 爆炸的力量如此之大，以至于所有窗户都碎了。

#### ⑧ 其他

| 情况 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 疑问句 | **Are you** a student? |
| here/there + be | **Here is** your book. 这是你的书。 |
| 频度副词 often/many a time 句首 | **Often did he** tell us stories. |
| well/badly 等程度副词句首 | **Well do I** remember it. |

### ✍️ 练习 37.2：部分倒装

改写：I have never seen this

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Never have I seen this.`

</details>

### 37.3 倒装口诀总结

```
否定副词放句首，部分倒装不用愁；
only 加状语在句首，同样倒装记心头；
so/neither 表"也"时，助动词提前莫忘掉；
as 让步须倒装，形副名动放前头；
虚拟条件省 if，were/had/should 到句首；
地点方位在句首，主语名词全倒装。
```

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **主语是代词时用完全倒装**：❌ Here comes he. → ✅ Here **he comes**.
2. **not until 从句也倒装**：❌ Not until did he come back... → ✅ **Not until he came back did I** leave.
3. **only 修饰主语时倒装**：❌ Only he does know. → ✅ **Only he knows**.
4. **as 让步从句名词提前加冠词**：❌ A child as he is → ✅ **Child as** he is
5. **so it is / so is it 混用**

### ✍️ 练习 37.3：倒装综合

判断：否定副词置于句首时通常需要部分倒装。

<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

## 38. 强调句

### 讲解

**强调句用来突出句子中的某个成分。**

### 38.1 It is/was + 被强调部分 + that/who + 其余部分

**这是最常用的强调结构。**

| 强调对象 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 原句 | **Tom** met **Mary** **in the park** **yesterday**. |
| 强调主语 | **It was Tom that/who** met Mary in the park yesterday. |
| 强调宾语 | **It was Mary that/who(m)** Tom met in the park yesterday. |
| 强调地点 | **It was in the park that** Tom met Mary yesterday. |
| 强调时间 | **It was yesterday that** Tom met Mary in the park. |

**规则：**
1. 强调**人**时可用 `that` 或 `who/whom`；强调**其他**只能用 `that`
2. 时态：原句是现在/将来 → 用 `It is`；原句是过去 → 用 `It was`
3. **不能强调谓语动词**（谓语用 do/does/did 强调）

### ✍️ 练习 38.1：强调句结构

强调 Tom

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`It was Tom that broke the window.`

</details>

### 38.2 强调句的判断方法（去掉法）

**把 `It is/was` 和 `that` 去掉，如果剩下的部分能组成完整的句子，就是强调句。**

- **It was** in the park **that** I met him. 我就是在公园里遇见他的。
  → 去掉后：In the park I met him. ✅ 完整 → 强调句
- **It is** important **that** we study hard. 我们努力学习是很重要的。
  → 去掉后：important we study hard. ❌ 不完整 → 不是强调句（是主语从句）

### ✍️ 练习 38.2：强调句判断

判断：去掉 It is/was...that 后句子仍完整，通常是强调句。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 38.3 强调句的疑问形式

| 类型 | 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 一般疑问 | **Is/Was it** + 被强调部分 + that...? | **Was it** Tom **that** broke the window? |
| 特殊疑问 | 疑问词 + **is/was it** + that...? | **Who was it that** broke the window? |
| | | **When was it that** you met him? 你是什么时候遇见他的？ |

### ✍️ 练习 38.3：强调句疑问

翻译：是谁发送了邮件？

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Who was it that sent the email?`

</details>

### 38.4 not...until 的强调句（高频）

**原句：** He **didn't** go to bed **until** his mother came back.
**强调：** **It was not until** his mother came back **that** he went to bed.

**⚠️ 注意：not 要跟着 until 一起提前，主句变肯定。**

### ✍️ 练习 38.4：not...until 强调

改写：I didn't reply until noon

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`It was not until noon that I replied.`

</details>

### 38.5 谓语动词的强调：do / does / did

**在动词原形前加 do/does/did，表示"确实、的确"：**

- I **do** love you. 我确实爱你。
- He **does** know the answer. 他的确知道答案。
- She **did** come yesterday. 她昨天确实来了。
- **Do** come in! 快请进！（祈使句加强语气）

**⚠️ 加了 do/does/did，后面动词用原形：**
- ❌ He did came. → ✅ He **did come**. 他确实来了。

### ✍️ 练习 38.5：谓语动词强调

强调：I understand.

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：I do understand. 中 do 表示确实。

</details>

### 38.6 其他强调方式

| 方式 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 反身代词 | I **myself** did it. |
| very / own | This is the **very** book I want. 这正是我想要的那本书。 / my **own** car |
| at all / in the world / on earth | What **on earth** are you doing? 你到底在做什么？ |
| indeed / really / certainly | He is **indeed** a good man. 他确实是个好人。 |
| 疑问词 + ever | **Whatever** do you mean? |
| 双重否定 | There is **no** rule **without** exceptions. |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **强调句和主语从句混淆**（用去掉法判断）
2. **强调句和定语从句混淆**：
   - **It was** the book **that** I bought.（可能是强调句，也可能是定语从句，看语境）
3. **not until 强调时主句忘记变肯定**
4. **do 后面动词没用原形**

### ✍️ 练习 38.6：其他强调方式

翻译：我自己完成了它

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I myself completed it.`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 38

改为强调句（强调加粗部分）：

1. **Tom** broke the window. 汤姆打破了窗户。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：**It was Tom that/who** broke the window.

</details>
2. I met him **in the park**. 我在公园里遇见了他。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：**It was in the park that** I met him.

</details>
3. He came **yesterday**.

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：**It was yesterday that** he came.

</details>
4. He didn't leave until his father came back.（用 It was not until...）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：**It was not until** his father came back **that** he left.

</details>

判断是否为强调句：

5. It is Tom that broke the window. 是汤姆打破了窗户。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：是强调句

</details>
6. It is certain that he will come. 他一定会来。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：不是（it 是形式主语，后接主语从句）

</details>
7. It was in 2010 that he graduated. 他就是在2010年毕业的。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：是强调句

</details>

用 do/does/did 强调：

8. I love you

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：I **do** love you.

</details>
9. He knows it

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：He **does** know it.

</details>
10. She came yesterday

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：She **did** come yesterday.

</details>

## 39. 省略句

### 讲解

**省略 = 为了避免重复，省去句子中不言自明的成分。**

### 39.1 简单句中的省略

| 情况 | 完整句 | 省略后 |
|---|---|---|
| 祈使句省主语 | (You) Come here. | **Come here.** |
| 答语省略 | Who did it? — (I did it.) | **I did.** |
| 省略主语 + be | (It is a) Pity! | **Pity!** |
| 感叹句省主谓 | (What a) Beautiful day (it is)! | **Beautiful day!** |
| 省略重复部分 | I like tea and (I like) coffee. | I like tea and coffee. |

### ✍️ 练习 39.1：简单句省略

补全：Want some coffee? = ____ you ____ some coffee?

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Do; want`

</details>

### 39.2 并列句中的省略

**省略重复的成分：**
- He can speak English, and she can (speak English), **too**. 他会说英语，她也会。 → and so can she.
- Tom went to Beijing and Mary (went) to Shanghai. 汤姆去了北京，玛丽去了上海。
- I'll go if you will (go). 如果你去，我就去。

### ✍️ 练习 39.2：并列句省略

判断：Tom likes tea and Mary coffee 中第二个 likes 被省略。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 39.3 复合句中的省略

#### ① 状语从句中的省略（重点）

**当从句主语与主句主语一致，且从句中有 be 动词时，可省略"主语 + be"。**

**适用连词：** when, while, if, unless, though, although, as if, once, whether

| 完整 | 省略 |
|---|---|
| **When he was** young, he lived in Beijing. 他年轻时住在北京。 | **When young**, he lived in Beijing. |
| **If it is** possible, come earlier. 如果可能的话，早点来。 | **If possible**, come earlier. |
| **Though he was** tired, he kept working. 尽管他很累，他还是继续工作。 | **Though tired**, he kept working. |
| **While she was** walking, she saw a bird. 她走路时看到了一只鸟。 | **While walking**, she saw a bird. |
| **Unless it is** necessary, don't call me. 除非必要，不要给我打电话。 | **Unless necessary**, don't call me. |

**常见省略短语：**
`if necessary, if possible, if any, if so, if not, when necessary, when in doubt, as usual, as planned, as required, though tired, unless invited`

#### ② 宾语从句中省略 that

- I know (that) he is right. 我知道他是对的。

#### ③ 定语从句中省略关系代词

- The book (which/that) I bought is nice.（作宾语时可省）

#### ④ 不定式省略 to（避免重复）

- — Would you like to go? — Yes, I'd like **to**.（省略 go）
- You may go if you want **to**. 如果你想去，你可以去。

**⚠️ 但 be / have 要保留：**
- — Are you a student? — Yes, I'd like **to be**.（保留 be）

### ✍️ 练习 39.3：复合句省略

改写：When it is necessary, call me.

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：When necessary, call me. = `When it is necessary, call me.`

</details>

### 39.4 常见省略结构

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| **if any** | Correct the mistakes, **if any**.（如果有的话） |
| **if ever** | He seldom, **if ever**, goes there.（即使有也很少） |
| **if so** | **If so**, I'll go with you.（如果那样） |
| **if not** | **If not**, tell me.（如果不是） |
| **why not do** | **Why not** go with us?（不如和我们一起去吧） |
| **what if** | **What if** it rains?（如果下雨怎么办） |
| **as follows** | The reasons are **as follows**. 理由如下。 |
| **as usual** | He came late, **as usual**. 他和往常一样迟到了。 |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **省略后主语不一致**：
   - ❌ **When young**, my mother took me there.（young 的主语应是"我"，但主句主语是"母亲"）
   - ✅ **When I was young**, my mother took me there. 我年轻的时候，我妈妈带我去了那里。
2. **不定式 to 后忘记省略动词**
3. **过度省略导致歧义**

### ✍️ 练习 39.4：常见省略

判断：I can help, if you want me to. 中 to 代替 help。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 39

省略句子中可省的部分：

1. When he was a child, he lived in Beijing. 他小时候住在北京。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：**When a child**, he lived in Beijing.

</details>
2. If it is possible, please come earlier. 如果可能的话，请早点来。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：**If possible**, please come earlier.

</details>
3. Though he was tired, he kept on working. 尽管他很累，他还是继续工作。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：**Though tired**, he kept on working.

</details>
4. The book which I bought is very good. 我买的那本书很好。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：The book **I bought** is very good.

</details>

补全省略的部分：

5. — Would you like to come? — Yes, I'd love ___. ——你想来吗？——是的，我很想。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：to

</details>
6. Correct the errors, ___ any. 如果有错误，请改正。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：if

</details>
7. He came late, ___ usual. 他和往常一样迟到了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：as

</details>

改错：

8. ❌ When young, my father often took me to the park.（意思：我小时候）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ **When I was young**, my father often took me to the park.

</details>

## 40. 独立主格结构

### 讲解

**独立主格 = 名词/代词 + 非谓语动词（或形容词/副词/介词短语），作状语，有自己的逻辑主语。**

**关键：它的逻辑主语与主句主语不同，所以叫"独立"。**

### 40.1 基本结构

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 名词/代词 + **现在分词** | **The meeting being over**, we all left. |
| 名词/代词 + **过去分词** | **His homework finished**, he went out. |
| 名词/代词 + **不定式** | **A lot of work to do**, I can't go out. |
| 名词/代词 + **形容词** | **The weather fine**, we went out. |
| 名词/代词 + **副词** | **The meal over**, we went home. |
| 名词/代词 + **介词短语** | **The teacher in front**, the students followed. |

### ✍️ 练习 40.1：独立主格结构

完成：The meeting over, we ____ (leave)

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`left`

</details>

### 40.2 独立主格 vs 分词作状语

| 对比 | 分词作状语 | 独立主格 |
|---|---|---|
| 逻辑主语 | **与主句主语一致** | **与主句主语不同** |
| 例句 | **Finishing** his work, he went home.（他做完工作） | **His work finished**, he went home.（工作完成了） |
| 例句 | **Being** ill, he stayed home. | **His mother being** ill, he stayed home. |

### ✍️ 练习 40.2：独立主格与分词

判断：Weather permitting 是独立主格结构。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 40.3 with 复合结构（超高频）

**结构：with + 宾语 + 宾补**

| 宾补形式 | 含义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| with + n. + **doing** | 主动、正在进行 | He came in **with a book in his hand**. |
| | | She sat there **with tears running down**. 她坐在那里，眼泪往下流。 |
| with + n. + **done** | 被动、已完成 | He slept **with the window open**. |
| | | He stood there **with his arms folded**. 他站在那里，双臂交叉。 |
| with + n. + **to do** | 将要做 | **With so much work to do**, I can't go. |
| with + n. + **形容词** | 状态 | He slept **with the door open**. |
| with + n. + **副词** | 状态 | He came in **with his hat on**. |
| with + n. + **介词短语** | 状态 | He entered **with a book under his arm**. |

**⚠️ doing 还是 done？看宾语与动词的主被动关系：**
- with the light **burning**（灯在燃烧 → 主动）
- with the door **locked**（门被锁 → 被动）
- with the work **to be done**（工作将被做）

**⚠️ without 复合结构：**
- He left **without saying** a word. 他一句话没说就离开了。

### ✍️ 练习 40.3：with 复合结构

翻译：他手里拿着文件走进来

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He came in with a file in his hand.`

</details>

### 40.4 独立主格的常见固定表达

| 表达 | 含义 |
|---|---|
| Weather permitting | 天气允许的话 |
| All things considered | 考虑到所有情况 |
| Generally speaking | 一般来说 |
| Judging from/by | 从……判断 |
| Strictly speaking | 严格来说 |
| Speaking of | 说到 |
| To be honest / frank | 老实说 |
| To tell the truth | 说实话 |
| Considering... | 考虑到 |
| Given... | 鉴于 |

**⚠️ 这些是"独立成分"，不受逻辑主语一致的限制：**
- **Generally speaking**, the weather is nice.（不需要主句主语是"人"）

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **独立主格和分词状语混用**（看逻辑主语是否一致）
2. **with 复合结构中 doing/done 用反**
3. **独立主格中忘记逻辑主语**：
   - ❌ **Being fine**, we went out.（谁 fine？）
   - ✅ **The weather being fine**, we went out. 天气晴朗，我们出去了。

### ✍️ 练习 40.4：固定表达

翻译：天气允许的话，我们出发

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Weather permitting, we will leave.`

</details>

选择或改写：

1. ____ (The work finished / The work finishing), we went home

<details>

答案：`The work finished`

</details>
2. Weather ____ (permitting / permitted), we will have the meeting outside

<details>

答案：`permitting`

</details>
3. 改写：`Because the weather was fine, we went hiking.`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The weather being fine, we went hiking.`

</details>
4. 判断：`The meeting being over, everyone left.` 中 `the meeting being over` 是独立主格结构

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：正确

</details>
5. 改错：`The weather permitting, we will goes outside.`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The weather permitting, we will go outside.`

</details>

## 41. 主谓一致

### 讲解

**主谓一致 = 谓语动词在人称和数上要与主语保持一致。**

三大原则：**语法一致、意义一致、就近一致**。

### 41.1 语法一致原则

**主语是单数 → 谓语单数；主语是复数 → 谓语复数。**

| 主语类型 | 谓语 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 单数名词/不可数名词 | 单数 | The book **is** here. / Water **is** important. |
| 复数名词 | 复数 | The books **are** here. |
| 动名词/不定式作主语 | **单数** | **Swimming is** good. / **To learn English takes** time. |
| 从句作主语 | **单数** | **What he said is** true. |
| 时间/距离/金额/重量作整体 | **单数** | **Ten years is** a long time. / **Five dollars is** enough. |

### ✍️ 练习 41.1：语法一致

填空：The list of tasks ____ (is / are) long

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`is`

</details>

### 41.2 and 连接的主语

| 情况 | 谓语 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 两个不同的人/物 | 复数 | Tom **and** Jack **are** friends. |
| 同一人/物（第二个词前无冠词） | **单数** | The **singer and dancer is** here.（一个人身兼两职） |
| 两个人/物（第二个词前有冠词） | 复数 | The **singer and the dancer are** here.（两个人） |
| 构成整体概念 | **单数** | **Bread and butter is** my breakfast. |
| each/every + n. and each/every + n. | **单数** | **Every** boy **and every** girl **likes** it. |

**构成整体的常见搭配：** bread and butter（黄油面包）, knife and fork（刀叉）, iron and steel（钢铁）, the horse and cart（马车）, trial and error（反复试验）

### ✍️ 练习 41.2：and 连接主语

填空：Tom and Anna ____ (work) here

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`work`

</details>

### 41.3 就近原则（谓语跟最近的主语一致）

| 结构 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| either...or... | **Either** you **or he is** right. 要么你对，要么他对。 |
| neither...nor... | **Neither** you **nor I am** right. 你我都不对。 |
| not only...but also... | **Not only** you **but also he is** right. 不仅你对，他也对。 |
| or | You **or he is** right. 你或他是对的。 |
| there be | **There is** a book and two pens. 那里有一本书和两支笔。 |
| here be | **Here are** two books and a pen. 这里有两本书和一支笔。 |

### ✍️ 练习 41.3：就近原则

填空：Neither the manager nor the assistants ____ (is / are) ready

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`are`

</details>

### 41.4 就远原则（谓语跟前面的主语一致）

**以下短语连接主语时，谓语与"前面"的主语一致：**

`with, together with, along with, as well as, besides, including, in addition to, rather than, no less than, like, but, except`

- Tom, **as well as** his friends, **is** coming.（看 Tom）
- The teacher, **together with** the students, **is** here. 老师和学生们都在这里。
- I, **rather than** you, **am** to blame. 该受责备的是我，而不是你。

### ✍️ 练习 41.4：就远原则

填空：The manager, as well as the assistants, ____ (is / are) ready

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`is`

</details>

### 41.5 意义一致原则

#### ① 集体名词

| 情况 | 谓语 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 看作**整体** | 单数 | My **family is** a big one. |
| 看作**成员** | 复数 | My **family are** all doctors. |

**常见集体名词：** family, class, team, group, government, audience, committee, public, crowd, staff

**⚠️ 通常用复数的名词：** `police, people, cattle, folk`
- The **police are** coming. 警察来了。

`youth` 要看含义：表示“年轻人群体”时可用复数，表示“青春期/年轻时期”时通常用单数：`The youth are active.` / `Youth is short.`

**⚠️ 通常用单数：** `news, physics, mathematics, the United States`；`politics` 和 `economics` 表示学科时也用单数。
- The **news is** exciting. 这个消息令人兴奋。
- **Physics is** difficult.
- **Politics is** an interesting subject.（学科）
- His **politics are** conservative.（政治观点，复数）
- **Economics is** difficult.（学科）
- The **economics of the project are** uncertain.（经济因素，复数）

#### ② 形式复数，意义单数

| 词 | 谓语 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 学科名（-ics） | 单数 | **Mathematics is** hard. |
| 国名（-s） | 单数 | **The United States is** a big country. |
| news | 单数 | **News is** important. |
| means（方法） | 单/复皆可 | Every **means has** been tried. / All **means have** been tried. |

#### ③ 形式单数，意义复数

**the + 形容词 = 一类人 → 复数**
- **The rich are** not always happy. 富人并不总是快乐的。
- **The poor need** help. 穷人需要帮助。

### ✍️ 练习 41.5：意义一致

填空：The police ____ (is / are) investigating

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`are`

</details>

### 41.6 特殊主语的一致

| 主语 | 谓语 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| each / every / either / neither + n. | **单数** | **Each** student **has** a book. |
| one of + 复数 | **单数** | **One of** the students **is** here. |
| a number of + 复数 | **复数**（"许多"） | **A number of** students **are** here. |
| the number of + 复数 | **单数**（"……的数量"） | **The number of** students **is** 50. |
| many a + 单数 | **单数** | **Many a** student **has** passed. |
| more than one + 单数 | **单数** | **More than one** student **has** come. |
| more than 两个以上 + 复数 | 复数 | **More than two** students **have** come. |
| 分数/百分数 + of + n. | **看 of 后的名词** | **Two thirds of** the students **are** boys.<br>**Two thirds of** the work **is** done. |
| most/some/all/half/rest + of + n. | **看 of 后的名词** | **Most of** the water **is** clean.<br>**Most of** the students **are** here. |
| none of + n. | 单/复皆可 | **None of** them **is/are** here. |
| plenty of / a lot of + n. | 看名词 | **A lot of** money **is** needed. |
| the + 形容词 | 复数 | **The young are** energetic. |
| this kind of + n. | 单数 | **This kind of** book **is** popular. |
| n. + of this kind | 看名词 | **Books of this kind are** popular. |

### ✍️ 练习 41.6：特殊主语

填空：Ten dollars ____ (is / are) enough

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`is`

</details>

### 41.7 定语从句中的主谓一致

**关系代词的谓语与先行词一致：**

- He is one of the **students who are** good at English.（先行词 students → 复数）
- He is **the only one** of the students **who is** good at English.（the only one → 单数）

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **a number of / the number of 混用**
2. **as well as 用就近原则**（应该是就远）
3. **集体名词的判断**
4. **分数 + of 看后面名词**
5. **news / physics 用复数**

### ✍️ 练习 41.7：定语从句中的一致

填空：She is one of the people who ____ (work) here

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`work`

</details>

## 42. 时态呼应

### 讲解

**时态呼应 = 主句和从句的时态要相互配合。**

### 42.1 基本规则

| 主句时态 | 从句时态 |
|---|---|
| **现在时/将来时** | 根据实际情况，任何时态都可以 |
| **过去时** | 通常退到**过去范畴**的时态；客观真理或现在仍成立的事实可保留现在时 |

**主句过去时 → 从句时态对应表：**

| 从句实际时间 | 用什么时态 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 与主句**同时** | 一般过去时 / 过去进行时 | He said he **was** busy. |
| 在主句**之前** | 过去完成时 | He said he **had finished**. |
| 在主句**之后** | 过去将来时 | He said he **would come**. |

### ✍️ 练习 42.1：时态呼应

转述：He says he ____ (be) busy

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`is`

</details>

### 42.2 例外情况（主句过去时，从句仍用现在时）

| 情况 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| **客观真理、自然规律** | The teacher said that the earth **is** round. |
| **现在仍然存在的习惯/事实** | He told me that he **goes** to work by bus.（他现在还这样） |
| **谚语、格言** | He said that practice **makes** perfect. |

### ✍️ 练习 42.2：时态例外

判断：客观真理在过去转述中通常仍用现在时。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 42.3 特殊时态搭配规则汇总

| 规则 | 说明 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **主将从现** | 时间/条件状语从句用现在时表将来 | I'll go when he **comes**. |
| **主过从过** | 主句过去，从句过去范畴 | He said he **was** busy. |
| **before / after** | 时间先后明确，都可用一般过去时 | He left **before** I arrived. |
| **since 引导的从句** | 主句用现在完成时，从句用一般过去时 | I **have lived** here **since** I **came** to Beijing. |
| **It is/has been + 时间 + since** | since 从句用过去时 | It **is** three years **since** he **left**. |
| **not...until** | 主从时态一致 | He **didn't** leave **until** she **came**. |
| **as soon as** | 主将从现 或 都用过去时 | I'll call you **as soon as** I **arrive**. |
| **by the time** | 主句用完成时 | **By the time** you **arrive**, I **will have left**. |
| **This is the first time that** | 用现在完成时 | This is the first time I **have been** here. |
| **That was the first time that** | 用过去完成时 | That was the first time I **had been** there. |
| **It is + 时间 + that**（强调） | 与原句时态一致 | **It was** yesterday **that** I saw him. |
| **hardly...when / no sooner...than** | 主句过去完成，从句一般过去 | **Hardly had** I arrived **when** it **rained**. |
| **would rather + 从句** | 用过去式（虚拟） | I'd rather you **came** tomorrow. |
| **wish + 从句** | 虚拟语气 | I wish I **were** rich. |

### ✍️ 练习 42.3：时态搭配

填空：He said he ____ (finish) before I arrived

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`had finished`

</details>

### 42.4 常见时态搭配练习

```
✅ He says he is busy.（现在 + 现在）
✅ He says he was busy.（现在 + 过去，也可以）
✅ He says he will come.（现在 + 将来）
✅ He said he was busy.（过去 + 过去）
❌ He said he is busy.（除非现在仍然忙）
✅ He said he had finished.（过去 + 过去的过去）
✅ He said he would come.（过去 + 过去将来）
✅ He said the earth is round.（真理，例外）
```

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **主句过去时，从句还用现在时**
2. **主将从现忘记**
3. **since 从句时态**
4. **This is the first time 后忘记用完成时**

### ✍️ 练习 42.4：时态呼应综合

翻译：她说她明天会来

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`She said she would come the next day.`

</details>

</details>

</details>

## 43. 直接引语与间接引语

### 讲解

**直接引语：** 直接引用别人的原话，用引号。
**间接引语：** 用自己的话转述，不用引号，变成宾语从句。

### 43.1 转换的四大变化

```
1️⃣ 人称变化
2️⃣ 时态变化
3️⃣ 时间/地点状语变化
4️⃣ 句型变化（语序、连接词）
```

### ✍️ 练习 43.1：引语转换总览

判断：间接引语通常需要检查人称、时态和时间地点。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 43.2 人称变化：一主二宾三不变

| 直接引语中的人称 | 变化规则 |
|---|---|
| 第一人称（I, we, my） | 变成**主句主语**的人称 |
| 第二人称（you, your） | 变成**主句宾语**的人称 |
| 第三人称（he, she, they） | **不变** |

- He said, "**I** am busy." 他说：“我很忙。” → He said **he** was busy. 他说他很忙。
- He said to me, "**You** are right." 他对我说：“你是对的。” → He told me **I** was right. 他告诉我说我是对的。
- He said, "**She** is my sister." 他说：“她是我的姐姐。” → He said **she** was his sister. 他说她是他的姐姐。

### ✍️ 练习 43.2：人称变化

转述：Tom said, “I am busy.”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Tom said that he was busy.`

</details>

### 43.3 时态变化（主句是过去时才变）

| 直接引语 | 间接引语 |
|---|---|
| 一般现在时 | 一般过去时 |
| 现在进行时 | 过去进行时 |
| 现在完成时 | 过去完成时 |
| 一般过去时 | 过去完成时 |
| 一般将来时 | 过去将来时 |
| 过去完成时 | 不变 |
| 情态动词 can/may/will | could/might/would |
| must | must / had to |

**⚠️ 不变时态的情况：**
1. 主句是**现在时**或**将来时**
2. 客观真理
3. 历史事件（有明确过去时间）
4. 从句有具体过去时间状语

### ✍️ 练习 43.3：时态变化

转换：`today`；`tomorrow`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`that day`；tomorrow → `the next day`。

</details>

### 43.4 时间地点状语变化

| 直接引语 | 间接引语 |
|---|---|
| now | then |
| today | that day |
| tonight | that night |
| tomorrow | the next day / the following day |
| the day after tomorrow | two days later / in two days' time |
| yesterday | the day before / the previous day |
| the day before yesterday | two days before |
| last week | the week before / the previous week |
| next week | the next week / the following week |
| ...ago | ...before |
| here | there |
| this | that |
| these | those |
| come | go |
| bring | take |

### ✍️ 练习 43.4：时间地点变化

转换：`here`；`this`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`there`；this → `that`。

</details>

### 43.5 各种句型的转换

#### ① 陈述句 → that 从句

- He said, "I am a teacher." 他说：“我是老师。” → He said (**that**) he **was** a teacher. 他说他是一名老师。

#### ② 一般疑问句 → whether/if 从句 + 陈述语序

- He asked, "Are you a student?" 他问：“你是学生吗？” → He asked **whether/if** I **was** a student. 他问我是不是学生。
- He said, "Do you like it?" 他说：“你喜欢它吗？” → He asked **if** I **liked** it. 他问我是否喜欢它。

#### ③ 特殊疑问句 → 疑问词 + 陈述语序

- He asked, "Where do you live?" 他问：“你住在哪里？” → He asked **where** I **lived**. 他问我住在哪里。
- He asked me, "What is your name?" 他问我：“你叫什么名字？” → He asked me **what** my name **was**. 他问我叫什么名字。

#### ④ 祈使句 → tell/ask/order + sb. + (not) to do

- He said to me, "Open the door." 他对我说：“把门打开。” → He **told me to open** the door. 他叫我把门打开。
- He said to me, "Don't be late." 他对我说：“别迟到。” → He **told me not to be** late. 他叫我不要迟到。
- He said, "Please help me." 他说：“请帮帮我。” → He **asked me to help** him. 他请求我帮助他。

**常用引述动词：** tell（告诉）, ask（请求）, order（命令）, advise（建议）, warn（警告）, beg（恳求）, remind（提醒）, invite（邀请）, forbid（禁止）

#### ⑤ 感叹句 → 用 what/how 或转述

- He said, "What a nice day!" 他说：“多好的天气啊！” → He said **what a nice day it was**. 他说那天天气多好。
- He said, "How beautiful!" 他说：“多美啊！” → He **exclaimed how beautiful it was**. 他感叹说那有多美。

#### ⑥ Let's 句型

- He said, "Let's go." 他说：“我们走吧。” → He **suggested (that) we (should) go**. 他建议我们走。 / He suggested **going**. 他建议走。

### ✍️ 练习 43.5：各种句型转换

转述命令：She said, “Open the file.”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`She told me to open the file.`

</details>

### 43.6 综合转换示例

**直接引语：**
> Tom said to me, "I bought this book here yesterday, and I will give it to you tomorrow."

**间接引语：**
> Tom told me (that) he **had bought** that book **there the day before**, and (that) he **would give** it to **me the next day**.

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **人称没变**
2. **时态没变或变错**
3. **疑问句用了疑问语序**：
   - ❌ He asked where did I live. → ✅ He asked **where I lived**. 他问我住在哪里。
4. **祈使句忘记用不定式**
5. **时间状语没变**

### ✍️ 练习 43.6：综合转换

转述：He said, “I will reply tomorrow.”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`He said he would reply the next day.`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 43

改为间接引语：

1. He said, "I am busy." 他说：“我很忙。”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：He said (that) he **was** busy.

</details>
2. She said to me, "Do you like English?" 她对我说：“你喜欢英语吗？”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：She asked me **whether/if I liked** English.

</details>
3. He asked, "Where do you live?" 他问：“你住在哪里？”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：He asked **where I lived**.

</details>
4. The teacher said to us, "Don't be late." 老师对我们说：“不要迟到。”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：The teacher **told us not to be** late.

</details>
5. He said, "I will come tomorrow." 他说：“我明天会来。”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：He said he **would come the next day**.

</details>
6. She said, "I saw him yesterday." 她说：“我昨天见到他了。”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：She said she **had seen** him **the day before**.

</details>
7. He said, "Please open the window." 他说：“请打开窗户。”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：He **asked me to open** the window.

</details>
8. The teacher said, "The earth moves around the sun." 老师说：“地球绕着太阳转。”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：The teacher said (that) the earth **moves** around the sun.（客观真理不变）

</details>

改错：

9. ❌ He asked me where did I live. 他问我住在哪里。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ He asked me **where I lived**.

</details>
10. ❌ He said he is a doctor. 他说他是一名医生。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ He said he **was** a doctor.

</details>

## 44. 并列句与复合句

### 讲解

按**结构**分，英语句子分三类：**简单句、并列句、复合句**（还有并列复合句）。

### 44.1 三种句子结构

| 类型 | 定义 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **简单句** | 只有一套主谓 | I like English. |
| **并列句** | 两个或多个简单句 + 并列连词 | I like English, **and** he likes math. |
| **复合句** | 主句 + 从句 | I know **that he is right**. |
| **并列复合句** | 并列句 + 从句 | I know he is right, **but** I don't agree. |

### ✍️ 练习 44.1：句子结构

判断：I was tired, but I finished the report. 是并列句。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 44.2 并列句的四种关系

| 关系 | 连词 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **并列/递进** | and, both...and, not only...but also, neither...nor | He is smart **and** he works hard. |
| **转折** | but, yet, while, however | He is poor, **but** he is happy. |
| **选择** | or, either...or, otherwise | Hurry up, **or** you'll be late. |
| **因果** | so, for, therefore, thus | It rained, **so** we stayed home. 下雨了，所以我们待在家里。 |

**⚠️ 分号也可以连接两个句子：**
- He is poor**;** he is happy. 他虽然穷，但他很快乐。
- He is poor**; however,** he is happy.（however 是副词，前面用分号）

### ✍️ 练习 44.2：并列关系

选择：I stayed late (because / but) the work was urgent

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`because`

</details>

### 44.3 三大从句总览

| 从句类型 | 作用 | 连接词 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **名词性从句** | 作主语、宾语、表语、同位语 | that, whether/if, wh-词 | I know **that he is right**. |
| **定语从句** | 修饰名词 | who, which, that, whose, when, where, why | The book **that I bought** is nice. |
| **状语从句** | 修饰动词/全句 | when, if, because, although... | I'll go **if it doesn't rain**. |

### ✍️ 练习 44.3：三大从句

判断：that he is right 在 I know that he is right 中作宾语从句。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 44.4 三大从句的判断技巧（超高频难点）

**看从句在句中的位置和作用：**

```
从句在名词后面？
   ├─ 从句解释名词的"内容"（名词 = 从句）→ 同位语从句
   │    The news that he won is true.（新闻 = 他赢了）
   └─ 从句修饰名词（"……的"）→ 定语从句
        The news that he told me is true.（他告诉我的新闻）

从句作句子的主/宾/表？→ 名词性从句
   What he said is true.（主语从句）

从句表示时间/原因/条件等？→ 状语从句
   I'll go if it's fine.
```

**经典对比：**

| 句子 | 从句类型 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|
| This is **the place where** we met. | 定语从句 | where 修饰 place |
| Put it **where** you found it. | 状语从句 | 前面没有先行词 |
| **Where** he went is unknown. | 主语从句 | where 从句作主语 |
| The reason is **that** he was ill. | 表语从句 | 在 be 后作表语 |
| The reason **why** he was late is unknown. | 定语从句 | why 修饰 reason |
| I don't know **what** he wants. | 宾语从句 | 作 know 的宾语 |
| I don't know **the book that** he wants. | 定语从句 | that 修饰 book |

### ✍️ 练习 44.4：判断从句功能

判断：修饰名词的从句通常是定语从句。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 44.5 that 的多种用法总结（终极对比）

| 用法 | 特点 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| 指示代词 | "那个" | **That** is my book. |
| 定语从句关系代词 | 在从句中**作成分**，可省 | The book **that** I bought... |
| 名词性从句连词 | **不作成分**，无义 | I know **that** he is right. |
| 同位语从句连词 | 解释抽象名词，不作成分 | The fact **that** he lied is clear. |
| 强调句 | It is...that... | **It was** Tom **that** did it. |
| so...that / such...that | 结果状语从句 | He is **so** tired **that**... |

### ✍️ 练习 44.5：that 的用法

判断：I know that he left 中 that 是宾语从句连接词。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 44.6 平行结构（写作要点）

**并列的成分必须结构一致：**

- ❌ I like swimming, running and **to read**. 我喜欢游泳、跑步和读书。
- ✅ I like swimming, running and **reading**. 我喜欢游泳、跑步和读书。
- ❌ He is smart, kind and **works hard**. 他聪明、善良而且工作努力。
- ✅ He is smart, kind and **hardworking**. 他聪明、善良而且勤奋。
- ❌ Not only he came, but also helped. 他不但来了，还帮了忙。
- ✅ **Not only did he come, but he also helped**. 他不但来了，还帮了忙。

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **两个句子直接用逗号连接**（逗号粘连）：
   - ❌ I like English, he likes math. 我喜欢英语，他喜欢数学。
   - ✅ I like English**, and** he likes math. 我喜欢英语，他喜欢数学。
   - ✅ I like English**;** he likes math. 我喜欢英语，他喜欢数学。
2. **however / therefore 当连词用**：
   - ❌ It rained, however we went out. 下雨了，然而我们还是出去了。
   - ✅ It rained**; however,** we went out. 下雨了，然而我们还是出去了。
   - ✅ It rained**. However,** we went out. 下雨了。然而，我们还是出去了。
3. **平行结构不一致**
4. **although + but 连用**

### ✍️ 练习 44.6：平行结构

改正：She likes reading, writing, and to speak

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`She likes reading, writing, and speaking.`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 44

判断句子类型（简单句/并列句/复合句）：

1. I like English and math. 我喜欢英语和数学。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：简单句

</details>
2. I like English, and he likes math. 我喜欢英语，他喜欢数学。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：并列句

</details>
3. I know that he likes English. 我知道他喜欢英语。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：复合句

</details>
4. The book that I bought is interesting. 我买的那本书很有趣。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：复合句

</details>

判断从句类型：

5. I don't know what he wants. 我不知道他想要什么。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：宾语从句

</details>
6. The book which I bought is nice. 我买的那本书很好。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：定语从句

</details>
7. I'll go if it doesn't rain. 如果不下雨，我就去。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：条件状语从句

</details>
8. The news that he won made us happy. 他获胜的消息让我们很高兴。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：同位语从句

</details>
9. The reason why he was late is unclear. 他迟到的原因不清楚。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：定语从句

</details>
10. Put it where you found it. 把它放回你找到它的地方。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：地点状语从句

</details>

改错：

11. ❌ I like English, he likes math. 我喜欢英语，他喜欢数学。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ I like English**, and** he likes math.

</details>
12. ❌ It rained, however we went out. 下雨了，然而我们还是出去了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ It rained**; however,** we went out.

</details>
13. ❌ He is smart, kind and works hard. 他聪明、善良而且工作努力。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ He is smart, kind and **hardworking**.

</details>

## 45. 标点与大小写

### 讲解

英文标点与中文有明显差异，写作中容易出错。

### 45.1 中英标点对照

| 标点 | 中文 | 英文 | 注意 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 句号 | 。 | **.** | 英文是点 |
| 逗号 | ， | **,** | 英文逗号后有空格 |
| 顿号 | 、 | **,** | 英文**没有顿号**，一律用逗号 |
| 引号 | "" | **" "** 或 **' '** | 英文引号形状不同 |
| 书名号 | 《》 | *斜体* 或 引号 | 英文**没有书名号** |
| 省略号 | …… | **...** | 英文三个点 |
| 破折号 | —— | **—** 或 **--** | |
| 冒号 | ： | **:** | |
| 分号 | ； | **;** | |

**⚠️ 中英标点不能混用！**
- ❌ I like apples，bananas、and oranges。
- ✅ I like apples**,** bananas**,** and oranges**.** 我喜欢苹果、香蕉和橙子。

### ✍️ 练习 45.1：中英标点

判断：英语句号使用半角 `.`。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 45.2 逗号的主要用法

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 并列成分之间 | I like apples**,** bananas**,** and oranges. |
| 连接并列句（and/but/or 前） | He is poor**,** but he is happy. |
| 从句在主句前时 | **If it rains,** I won't go. |
| 非限制性定语从句 | Tom**,** who is my friend**,** is a doctor. |
| 插入语前后 | He**,** however**,** disagreed. |
| 状语放句首 | **Yesterday,** I met him. |
| 直接引语前 | He said**,** "I'm busy." |
| 同位语 | Tom**,** my friend**,** is here. |

**⚠️ 从句在主句后面时，一般不加逗号：**
- ✅ **If it rains,** I won't go.（从句在前，加逗号）
- ✅ I won't go **if it rains**.（从句在后，不加逗号）

### ✍️ 练习 45.2：逗号

给并列句补标点

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：I was tired, but I continued.

</details>

### 45.3 撇号（'）的用法

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 所有格 | Tom**'s** book, the students**'** books |
| 缩写 | don**'**t, it**'**s, I**'**m, they**'**re |
| 字母/数字的复数 | Mind your p**'s** and q**'s**. |

**⚠️ its / it's 千万别混：**
- `its` = 它的（所有格，**没有撇号**）
- `it's` = it is / it has

### ✍️ 练习 45.3：撇号

选择：the (students' / student's) books（多个学生的书）

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`students'`

</details>

### 45.4 引号的用法

| 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|
| 直接引语 | He said, **"**I'm busy.**"** |
| 文章/歌曲/诗歌标题 | I read **"**The Road Not Taken**"**. |
| 特殊含义/讽刺 | His **"**friend**"** betrayed him. |

**⚠️ 美式英语中，句号和逗号放引号**里面**：**
- ✅ He said, "I'm busy**."** 他说：“我很忙。”
- ✅ "I'm busy**,"** he said. “我很忙，”他说。

### ✍️ 练习 45.4：引号

标出直接引语

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：She said, “I am ready.”

</details>

### 45.5 冒号与分号

| 标点 | 用法 | 例句 |
|---|---|---|
| **冒号 :** | 引出列举 | I need three things**:** a pen, paper, and ink. |
| | 引出解释 | He had one goal**:** to win. |
| | 引出引语（正式） | He said**:** "Let's go." |
| **分号 ;** | 连接两个相关的独立句 | He is poor**;** he is happy. |
| | 分隔含逗号的列举项 | I visited Paris, France**;** Rome, Italy**;** and Tokyo, Japan. |
| | 连接副词前（however, therefore） | It rained**;** however, we went out. |

### ✍️ 练习 45.5：冒号与分号

判断：分号可以连接两个关系密切的独立分句。


<details data-answer-key>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

- 第 1 题：正确

</details>

### 45.6 大写规则

| 情况 | 例子 |
|---|---|
| 句首字母 | **T**his is a book. |
| 人名 | **T**om **S**mith |
| 地名/国名 | **C**hina, **B**eijing, the **G**reat **W**all |
| 星期/月份/节日 | **M**onday, **J**anuary, **C**hristmas |
| 语言/民族 | **E**nglish, **C**hinese |
| 书名/标题（实词首字母） | **T**he **O**ld **M**an and the **S**ea |
| 代词 I | **I** am here. |
| 缩写 | **UN**, **USA**, **TV**, **CEO** |
| 直接引语首字母 | He said, "**W**e are ready." |
| 头衔（在名字前） | **P**resident Lincoln, **D**r. Smith |

**⚠️ 季节不大写：** ✅ spring, summer, autumn, winter
**⚠️ 学科一般不大写（语言除外）：** ✅ math, physics, history；但 ✅ **E**nglish

### ✍️ 练习 45.6：大写规则

改正：i work in london

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I work in London.`

</details>

### 45.7 常见标点错误

| 错误 | 正确 |
|---|---|
| ❌ 逗号粘连：I like tea, he likes coffee. | ✅ I like tea**, and** he likes coffee. |
| ❌ 中文标点：I like it。 | ✅ I like it**.** |
| ❌ 用顿号：apples、bananas | ✅ apples**,** bananas |
| ❌ its' | ✅ **its**（所有格无撇号） |
| ❌ 逗号后无空格：apples,bananas | ✅ apples**,** bananas |
| ❌ i am a student | ✅ **I** am a student. 我是学生。 |

### ⚠️ 易错点

1. **中英标点混用**
2. **逗号粘连**（用逗号连接两个完整句子）
3. **its / it's 混用**
4. **代词 I 不大写**
5. **月份、星期不大写**

### ✍️ 练习 45.7：标点综合

改正：Although it was late, but we continued

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Although it was late, we continued.`

</details>

### ✍️ 章节复习测试 45

改正标点和大小写错误：

1. ❌ i like apples、bananas and oranges。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ I like apples**,** bananas and oranges**.**

</details>
2. ❌ he said "i am busy". 他说：“我很忙。”

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ **H**e said**, "I** am busy**."**

</details>
3. ❌ I like tea, he likes coffee. 我喜欢茶，他喜欢咖啡。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ I like tea**, and** he likes coffee.

</details>
4. ❌ The dog wagged it's tail. 狗摇了摇尾巴。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ The dog wagged **its** tail.

</details>
5. ❌ I was born on monday, may 5th. 我出生于五月五日星期一。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ I was born on **M**onday, **M**ay 5th.

6. ❌ It rained, however we went out. 下雨了，然而我们还是出去了。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

</details>
7. ❌ tom's book is on the desk。

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：✅ **T**om's book is on the desk**.**

</details>

## 🌐 第四阶段综合任务：提出并评估一项改进建议

> **任务目标：** 阅读正式文本、识别作者立场，并用清楚自然的英语提出建议。高级结构以准确和必要为原则，不以数量为目标。

### 1. 听力：团队会议摘要

请让学习伙伴朗读下文，或用文本转语音工具播放两遍。第一遍记录主要问题和建议，第二遍记录支持与保留意见。

> The team has suggested introducing two meeting-free afternoons each week. Supporters argue that employees would complete focused work more efficiently if fewer meetings were scheduled. Others agree with the goal but warn that urgent decisions might be delayed. It was therefore proposed that the policy be tested for one month before a final decision is made.
>
> 团队建议每周设置两个无会议的下午。支持者认为，如果少安排一些会议，员工就能更高效地完成需要专注的工作。其他人认同这一目标，但提醒紧急决定可能会被推迟。因此，有人提议先试行一个月，再作最终决定。

听后回答：What change has been suggested?（提出了什么改变？）What benefit and risk are mentioned?（文中提到了什么好处和风险？）Why will the policy be tested first?（为什么要先试行这项政策？）

### 2. 阅读：分析立场与结构

标出建议、支持理由、反方顾虑和折中方案。找出一处虚拟条件句、一处被动语态和一处形式主语结构，并用更简单的英语改写它们。

### 3. 口语：两分钟建议陈述

选择一个熟悉的问题，例如会议过多、学习时间不足或公共空间浪费。按“问题—原因—建议—风险—折中方案”的顺序发言2至3分钟，然后回答至少两个追问。

可使用：`I suggest that...`、`If we were to..., ...`、`One concern is that...`、`What matters most is...`

### 4. 写作：正式建议邮件

写一封180至220词的正式邮件。邮件应包括主题、背景、明确建议、两条理由、一个潜在风险、应对办法和礼貌结尾。完成初稿后，再删除不必要的倒装、强调或过长句。

**参考结构：** `I am writing to propose...` → `This would...` → `One possible concern is...` → `This risk could be reduced by...` → `I would welcome the opportunity to discuss...`

**达标标准：** 能区分事实、观点和建议；口语结构完整并能回答追问；邮件语体一致、论证连贯；复杂结构没有影响清晰度，基础检查正确率达到85%。

# 核心语法补充专题：助动词、限定词与语体

前面的 45 章建立了主干。本专题补充几个在真实阅读和写作中很常见、但不适合单独扩成新章节的知识点。

## 补充专题 1：助动词与疑问、否定

英语一般需要借助助动词构成疑问句和否定句。常见助动词有 `be`、`do` 和 `have`；情态动词 `can`、`should`、`must` 等本身也能承担助动词作用。

| 原句类型 | 疑问句 | 否定句 |
|---|---|---|
| be 动词 | **Is** she ready? | She **isn't** ready. |
| 一般动词 | **Does** she work here? | She **doesn't work** here. |
| 完成时 | **Has** she finished? | She **hasn't finished**. |
| 情态动词 | **Can** she swim? | She **can't swim**. |

一般动词前面已经有 `do/does/did` 时，后面的动词用原形：`Does he like tea?`，不能写成 `Does he likes tea?`。

### ✍️ 练习 补充 1.1：助动词

改错：`Did she went home?`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Did she go home?`

</details>

## 补充专题 2：限定词与数量表达

限定词放在名词前，帮助说明“是哪一个、多少个、谁的”。冠词、指示词、物主词和许多数量词都属于这一类。

| 表达 | 后面通常接 | 例子 |
|---|---|---|
| `this/that` | 单数可数名词或不可数名词 | this book, that advice |
| `these/those` | 复数可数名词 | these books |
| `each/every` | 单数可数名词 | every student |
| `many/few` | 复数可数名词 | many questions |
| `much/little` | 不可数名词 | much time |
| `some/any` | 可数复数或不可数名词 | some ideas, any water |
| `another` | 单数可数名词 | another day |
| `other` | 复数可数或不可数名词 | other people, other information |

`each` 强调一个一个地看，`every` 强调整体中的每一个；两者后面都接单数可数名词：`Each student has a book.`

### ✍️ 练习 补充 2.1：数量词

选择：There isn't (many / much) information here

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`much`

</details>

## 补充专题 3：关系从句的简化

关系从句有时可以缩短，使句子更紧凑。主动关系从句常可改为现在分词，进行中的被动关系从句常可改为过去分词。

- The students **who live** nearby walk to school. 住在附近的学生走路上学。
- The students **living** nearby walk to school. 住在附近的学生走路上学。
- The books **which were written** by Orwell are popular. 奥威尔写的书很受欢迎。
- The books **written** by Orwell are popular. 奥威尔写的书很受欢迎。

只有当关系代词在从句中作主语、且逻辑关系清楚时，才适合这样简化。不要把所有 `who/which` 都直接删除。

### ✍️ 练习 补充 3.1：简化关系从句

改写：The man who is standing by the door is my teacher

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`The man standing by the door is my teacher.`

</details>

## 补充专题 4：非谓语形式的意义区别

`to do`、`doing` 和 `done` 不只是形式不同，它们还表达不同的时间关系和语态。

| 形式 | 常见意义 | 例子 |
|---|---|---|
| `to do` | 目的、将来或尚未完成 | I went there **to help** him. |
| `doing` | 主动、同时或一般行为 | **Knowing** the truth, she stayed silent. |
| `done` | 被动或已经完成 | **Surprised** by the news, he said nothing. |
| `having done` | 主动且先于主句完成 | **Having finished** work, she went home. |

判断非谓语的逻辑主语时，通常要看它和主句主语是否一致：`Walking home, I saw a dog.` 中走路和看见的人都是 `I`。

### ✍️ 练习 补充 4.1：非谓语意义

选择：____ (Finish / Having finished) the report, she sent it to her manager

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`Having finished`

</details>

## 补充专题 5：正式语体与口语语体

同一个语法意思，在口语和正式写作中可能有不同表达。语法正确不等于所有场合都自然。

| 口语中常见 | 正式写作中常见 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `I'm` / `can't` | `I am` / `cannot` | 正式文章少用缩写 |
| `a lot of` | `many/much` | 正式写作更常用精确数量词 |
| `get` | `obtain/receive/become` | 根据具体含义选择，不要机械替换 |
| `Who did you talk to?` | `To whom did you speak?` | 后者正式，但日常口语较少用 |

正式表达不一定更“高级”。日常交流优先选择自然清楚的句子，考试和正式写作则要注意语体一致。

### ✍️ 练习 补充 5.1：语体选择

正式邮件中选择：`I am writing to ask about...` / `I'm just asking about...`

<details>
<summary>查看答案</summary>

答案：`I am writing to ask about...`

</details>

## 附录 A：中国学生 60 大易错点总表

> 这 60 条是中国学生最容易犯的错误。建议打印出来，每周过一遍。

### A1. 词性与词形（1–10）

| # | ❌ 错误 | ✅ 正确 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | She sings very beautiful. | She sings very **beautifully**. | 修饰动词用副词 |
| 2 | He treats me friendly. | He treats me **in a friendly way**. | friendly 是形容词 |
| 3 | He is very interesting in music. | He is very **interested** in music. | 人用 -ed，物用 -ing |
| 4 | The film is very interested. | The film is very **interesting**. | 同上 |
| 5 | He is an afraid boy. | He is a **frightened** boy. | afraid 不作前置定语 |
| 6 | a five-years-old boy | a five-**year**-old boy | 复合形容词中名词用单数 |
| 7 | two hundreds students | two **hundred** students | 具体数字后 hundred 不加 s |
| 8 | the twelveth | the **twelfth** | 序数词拼写 |
| 9 | He hardly works. | He works **hard**.（若表"努力"） | hard/hardly 意思完全不同 |
| 10 | He doesn't hardly know it. | He **hardly** knows it. | hardly 本身是否定词 |

### A2. 名词与冠词（11–18）

| # | ❌ 错误 | ✅ 正确 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 11 | some advices | some **advice** | 不可数名词无复数 |
| 12 | many furnitures | **much furniture** | 同上 |
| 13 | The news are exciting. | The news **is** exciting. | news 是不可数 |
| 14 | He is teacher. | He is **a** teacher. | 单数可数名词前必须有限定词 |
| 15 | I go to the bed at ten. | I go to **bed** at ten. | 抽象用法零冠词 |
| 16 | play the basketball | play **basketball** | 球类前不加 the |
| 17 | play piano | play **the** piano | 乐器前加 the |
| 18 | by a bus | **by bus** | 交通方式零冠词 |

### A3. 代词（19–24）

| # | ❌ 错误 | ✅ 正确 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 19 | Between you and I | Between you and **me** | 介词后用宾格 |
| 20 | Me and Tom are friends. | **Tom and I** are friends. | 主格 + 礼貌顺序 |
| 21 | The dog wags it's tail. | The dog wags **its** tail. | its 是所有格 |
| 22 | Everyone are ready. | Everyone **is** ready. | 不定代词用单数 |
| 23 | another books | **other books** / **another book** | another + 单数 |
| 24 | I want new something. | I want **something new**. | 形容词后置修饰不定代词 |

### A4. 动词与时态（25–40）

| # | ❌ 错误 | ✅ 正确 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 25 | He don't like it. | He **doesn't** like it. | 三单 |
| 26 | Does she likes it? | Does she **like** it? | 助动词后用原形 |
| 27 | Are you like music? | **Do** you like music? | 实义动词用 do |
| 28 | I very like English. | I like English **very much**. | very 不修饰动词 |
| 29 | He is study English. | He is study**ing** English. | 进行时结构 |
| 30 | I am knowing the answer. | I **know** the answer. | 状态动词无进行时 |
| 31 | Did you went there? | Did you **go** there? | did 后用原形 |
| 32 | I have saw it. | I have **seen** it. | 过去分词 |
| 33 | If it will rain, I won't go. | If it **rains**, I won't go. | 主将从现 |
| 34 | He said he is busy. | He said he **was** busy. | 主过从过 |
| 35 | I have seen him yesterday. | I **saw** him yesterday. | 完成时不与明确过去时间连用 |
| 36 | He has died for 3 years. | He **has been dead** for 3 years. | 非延续性动词 |
| 37 | He has gone to Paris.（人已回来） | He **has been to** Paris. | been to / gone to |
| 38 | since three years | **for** three years | since 接时间点 |
| 39 | He is used to get up early. | He is used to **getting** up early. | to 是介词 |
| 40 | He used to getting up early. | He **used to get** up early. 他过去习惯早起。 | used to do |

### A5. 被动与非谓语（41–48）

| # | ❌ 错误 | ✅ 正确 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 41 | The accident was happened. | The accident **happened**. | 不及物动词无被动 |
| 42 | The war was broken out. | The war **broke out**. | 同上 |
| 43 | He was made work. | He was made **to work**. | 被动中还原 to |
| 44 | The book is sold well. | The book **sells** well. | 主动表被动 |
| 45 | He let me to go. | He let me **go**. | 使役动词接原形 |
| 46 | I finished to do it. | I finished **doing** it. 我做完了这件事。 | finish + doing |
| 47 | He is good at play ball. | He is good at **playing** ball. | 介词后用动名词 |
| 48 | Walking in the street, a car hit me. | Walking in the street, **I** was hit by a car. | 垂悬分词 |

### A6. 从句（49–56）

| # | ❌ 错误 | ✅ 正确 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 49 | I know where is he. | I know **where he is**. | 宾语从句用陈述语序 |
| 50 | The book which I bought it | The book **which I bought** | 关系词已作宾语 |
| 51 | Tom, that is my friend, ... | Tom, **who** is my friend, ... | 非限制性不用 that |
| 52 | This is the house which I live. | This is the house **where** I live. | 从句成分完整用 where |
| 53 | The reason is because... | The reason is **that**... | 表语从句用 that |
| 54 | Although..., but... | **Although**..., ... | 两者不连用 |
| 55 | Because..., so... | **Because**..., ... | 两者不连用 |
| 56 | No matter what you say is right. | **Whatever** you say is right. | 名词性从句不用 no matter |

### A7. 虚拟、倒装与其他（57–60）

| # | ❌ 错误 | ✅ 正确 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 57 | If I was you... | If I **were** you... | 虚拟语气 be 用 were |
| 58 | I wish I am rich. | I wish I **were** rich. | wish 后虚拟 |
| 59 | Here comes he! | **Here he comes**! | 主语是代词不倒装 |
| 60 | I like tea, he likes coffee. | I like tea**, and** he likes coffee. | 逗号粘连 |

| learn | learnt/learned | learnt/learned | 学 |
| smell | smelt/smelled | smelt/smelled | 闻 |
| spell | spelt/spelled | spelt/spelled | 拼写 |

### B4. ABC 型（三态不同，最难，重点背）

| 原形 | 过去式 | 过去分词 | 含义 |
|---|---|---|---|
| be(am/is/are) | was/were | been | 是 |
| begin | began | begun | 开始 |
| drink | drank | drunk | 喝 |
| ring | rang | rung | 响 |
| sing | sang | sung | 唱 |
| sink | sank | sunk | 下沉 |
| swim | swam | swum | 游泳 |
| blow | blew | blown | 吹 |
| grow | grew | grown | 生长 |
| know | knew | known | 知道 |
| throw | threw | thrown | 扔 |
| fly | flew | flown | 飞 |
| draw | drew | drawn | 画 |
| show | showed | shown | 展示 |
| break | broke | broken | 打破 |
| choose | chose | chosen | 选择 |
| freeze | froze | frozen | 冻结 |
| speak | spoke | spoken | 说 |
| steal | stole | stolen | 偷 |
| wake | woke | woken | 醒 |
| drive | drove | driven | 驾驶 |
| ride | rode | ridden | 骑 |
| rise | rose | risen | 上升 |
| write | wrote | written | 写 |
| bite | bit | bitten | 咬 |
| hide | hid | hidden | 藏 |
| eat | ate | eaten | 吃 |
| fall | fell | fallen | 落下 |
| forget | forgot | forgotten | 忘记 |
| forgive | forgave | forgiven | 原谅 |
| give | gave | given | 给 |
| take | took | taken | 拿 |
| mistake | mistook | mistaken | 弄错 |
| shake | shook | shaken | 摇 |
| go | went | gone | 去 |
| do | did | done | 做 |
| see | saw | seen | 看见 |
| lie（躺） | lay | lain | 躺 |
| wear | wore | worn | 穿 |
| tear | tore | torn | 撕 |
| swear | swore | sworn | 发誓 |

### B5. 易混淆动词（必须分清）

| 原形 | 过去式 | 过去分词 | 含义 | 及物性 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **lie** | lay | lain | 躺 | 不及物 |
| **lie** | lied | lied | 说谎 | 不及物 |
| **lay** | laid | laid | 放置、下蛋 | **及物** |
| **rise** | rose | risen | 升起 | 不及物 |
| **raise** | raised | raised | 举起、提高 | **及物** |
| **sit** | sat | sat | 坐 | 不及物 |
| **seat** | seated | seated | 使就座 | **及物** |
| **fall** | fell | fallen | 落下 | 不及物 |
| **fell** | felled | felled | 砍倒 | **及物** |
| **find** | found | found | 找到 | 及物 |
| **found** | founded | founded | 建立 | 及物 |

**记忆口诀：**
```
躺 lie-lay-lain，放 lay-laid-laid；
撒谎 lie-lied-lied，别搞乱；
升 rise-rose-risen，举 raise 全加 -d；
不及物的没宾语，及物后面跟东西。
```

---

9. ✅ **Here he comes**!
10. ✅ **Only he knows** the answer. 只有他知道答案。
11. ✅ **Child as** he is, he knows a lot. 尽管他还是个孩子，他懂得很多。


</details>

<summary>查看答案</summary>
1. **It was Tom that/who** broke the window. 是汤姆打破了窗户。
2. **It was in the park that** I met him. 我就是在公园里遇见他的。
3. **It was yesterday that** he came. 他就是昨天来的。
4. **It was not until** his father came back **that** he left. 直到他父亲回来他才离开。
5. 是强调句 6. 不是（it 是形式主语，后接主语从句） 7. 是强调句
8. I **do** love you. 我确实爱你。 9. He **does** know it. 他确实知道。 10. She **did** come yesterday. 她确实昨天来了。


</details>

## 🎓 学完之后

恭喜你走完了 45 章、4 个阶段综合任务、核心语法补充专题和 3 个附录！

**接下来建议：**

1. **循环复习**：附录 A 的 60 条易错点每周过一遍
2. **输出训练**：每天用当天学的语法点造 5 个句子
3. **真实输入**：开始读英文文章，遇到复杂句就拆解成分
4. **不要死抠语法**：语法是工具，多听多说才能内化

**判断句子对错的基础检查法：**

```
① 每个分句的主语和谓语完整、匹配吗？
② 时态符合时间关系和表达意图吗？
③ 主动或被动符合主语的角色吗？
④ 名词的冠词、单复数和限定词合适吗？
⑤ 语序、搭配、连接词和标点自然吗？
```

祝你学有所成！🎉
